`docs/tersafe_strings.md`:

```md
# libtersafe 主字符串表：结构、参数族与全量重建

本文记录 `libtersafe.so`（ACE 主库，5,598,144 B，MD5 `E06344E0B5F6B09D13820C625FA3A123`）主字符串表的完整还原过程与结果。表中存放 ACE 的路径、属性名、模块名、协议字段与格式串；此前只能静态读到其中的明文部分，本次做到了**离线全量解密**。

## 1. 定位

| 项 | 值 |
| --- | --- |
| 表基址 | `0xFBCB0`（`.rodata`，由 `sub_4E4A68` 的 `adrp x0,#0xFB000; add x0,x0,#0xCB0` 得到） |
| 表上限 | `0x10C234`（`.rodata` 结束，紧随 `.eh_frame_hdr`） |
| 有效长度 | 66,948 B，其中密文记录止于约 60,947 |
| 明文缓存 | `sub_4E4A7C` 返回 `0x568FC4`（`.bss` 区，运行时写入，记录级缓存：首字节置 1 表示已解） |
| 记录数 | 3,198 条（旧报告记 2,868，见 §6） |

`.rodata` 段为 `0x90EC0 + 0x7B374`；表尾之后（`0xFBCB0+0x4F84` 起）是 C++ typeinfo 等明文数据，不属于表。

## 2. 记录格式

每条记录：

```
[key:u8][len_xor_key:u8][cipher:len][check:u8]
```

- `key = A[off]`
- `len = A[off+1] ^ key`（`len` 上限实测 118，超过 180 视为越界）
- 密文自 `A[off+2]` 起 `len` 字节
- `check = A[off+2+len]`

解密：

```
k_0 = key
for j in 0..len-1:
    plain[j] = cipher[j] ^ k_j
    k_{j+1} = (((k_j + j) & 0xFF) ^ E) + C   (mod 256)
```

`(E, C)` 是每条记录所属封印装函数的私有参数，全库共 99–101 组。

## 3. 参数族

解密封装函数位于 `0x4E64D0..0x4ED3CC`（旧报告记 100 个），每个函数形如：

```asm
mov w20, w0          ; idx
bl  0x4e4a68         ; A = 表基址
bl  0x4e4a7c         ; B = 明文缓存
mov w15, #E          ; 参数 E
add w1, w1, #C       ; 参数 C
```

因此 `(E, C)` 可直接从指令流静态提取：扫描 `mov w15, #imm` 后接 `add w1, w1, #imm`。实测提取到 75 组，其余由"失败记录的高频候选"补全（阈值：同一 `(E,C)` 在 ≥8 条校验失败的记录上出现），最终 99–101 组，与封装函数数量吻合。

参数分布有规律：同一族内 `E + C` 恒为 7 的倍数（14/21/28/…/105），`E` 覆盖 0–99。这也解释了为什么很多单字节记录对全部参数都解出同一结果（`len=1` 时递推不参与）。

## 4. 校验与消歧

封装函数尾部对明文做校验：

```asm
cs = 0xFF
for b in plain: cs ^= b
if (check ^ cs ^ key) != 0xFF:  标记失败
```

该式只提供 8 位区分度，因此：

- 长串（≥8 字节）几乎唯一确定；
- 短串（1–4 字节）会出现多个参数同时通过的情况，此时用语义打分（可打印字符、大小写、路径/格式符权重）取最优；
- 3,198 条中 1,298 条对候选集合给出**唯一明文**，其余按打分落定，无解 7 条、不可打印 0 条。

已知锚点全部命中，可认为重建正确：

| off | 明文 | 出处 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 49995 | `ERR` | `partD` 的 3 号槽 ErrKey；`sub_4ECF50(0xC34B)` |
| 50001 | `fake_token_androidid` | 17 槽回落来源 |
| 50303 | `x:%s;q:%s;Q:%s;o:%s` | fake token 主体格式 |
| 50325 | `%s;bt:%s;bd:%s;` | fake token 外层包装 |
| 50343 | `td:%s;bt:%s;bd:%s` | 无前缀变体 |
| 51374 | `Fake_Token_TDM_info` | provider 键 |
| 51396 / 51402 / 51410 / 51420 | `xid` / `QiMaM` / `QIMEI36` / `OsEz` | TDM_info 的四个子键（`QiMaM`=QIMEI、`OsEz`=OAID，键名被改名混淆） |
| 52998 | `AndroidIdErr` | `sub_2C9D78` 的 17 槽 |
| 58581 | `ft_oaid` | 17 槽首选来源 |
| 51301 | `/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id` | `sub_2CCE84` |
| 57640 | `ro.com.cph.mac_address` | `sub_2B6040`（oppo 系 MAC 属性） |

## 5. 引用点

`tools/tersafe_usage.py` 线性扫 `.text`，在 `bl` 目标落入 `0x4E64D0..0x4ED3CC` 时回溯最近的 `mov w0, #idx`，并用 `.eh_frame_hdr` 恢复的函数起点归组。结果：

- 调用点 3,246 处
- 不同 idx 2,110 个（占 3,198 条的 66%）
- 覆盖 876 个函数

产出 `docs/strings_usage.tsv`（`func / call / idx / string`）与 `docs/strings.tsv`（`offset / key / len / E / C / string`）。

高密度引用函数（前几名）：`sub_4E3CAC` 101 次、`sub_230C78` 64 次、`sub_1BCF84` 47 次、`sub_269EFC` 40 次、`sub_2CB31C` 39 次（Camera 采集）、`sub_27FAE4` 37 次（模块注册表）、`sub_28EAF4` 32 次、`sub_4C63CC` 32 次。

## 6. 与旧报告的差异

| 项 | 旧报告 | 本次实测 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 记录数 | 2,868 | 3,198 |
| 密文表尾 | 约 60,586 | 60,947（最后一条非空记录） |
| 参数组数 | 98 | 99–101（提取 75 + 频次补全 24–26） |
| 表基址 | 0xFBCB0（正确） | 0xFBCB0 |

旧报告 2,868 应是把尾部若干条（含 `ft_oaid`、`AndroidIdErr`）漏掉后的计数；本表按 3,198 条重建，`ft_oaid`（58,581）在旧表范围外，这正是此前 `0x2CC6C4` 首行显示空串的原因。

## 7. 复现

```powershell
cd sgame-ace-analysis\tools
python tersafe_strings.py ..\..\work\libs\libtersafe.so 0xFBCB0 66948 0x4E64D0 0x4ED3CC 4000 ..\..\work\out\tersafe_strings.tsv
python tersafe_usage.py  ..\..\work\libs\libtersafe.so 0x1b6c20 0x50dfc8 0x4E64D0 0x4ED3CC ..\..\work\out\tersafe_strings.tsv ..\..\work\out\tersafe_usage.tsv
```

第 4/5 参是参数扫描区间，第 6 参是记录上限（给 4000 即可覆盖全表）。

## 8. 尚未覆盖

- **内联串 945 条**：不在这张表里，由 262 个内联解码器就地解密，算法为 `p[i]=c[i]^((k+i)&0xFF)`，`k` 固定（已见 0x8A/0x91/0xC6/0xE8/0xAB/0xE5）。`ft_oaid` 之外的 OAID 相关常量、部分云控键在这一套里。
- **7 条无解记录**：参数不在 0–127 / C 0–7 的搜索空间内，属于未提取到的封装函数。
- **3 条空串调用点**：`idx` 落在记录中间（内联串编码），已被 usage 表排除。

```

import sys, json
CG = json.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\cg.json", encoding="utf-8"))
REV = {}
for k, v in CG.items():
    for c in v.get("i", []):
        try:
            c = int(c, 0) if isinstance(c, str) else int(c)
        except Exception:
            continue
        REV.setdefault(c, []).append(int(k, 0) if isinstance(k, str) else int(k))

def hx(x):
    return "%08x" % x

a = sys.argv[1:]
if a[0] == "-r":
    for t in a[1:]:
        t = int(t, 0)
        print("callers of %s: %s" % (hx(t), " ".join(hx(x) for x in sorted(set(REV.get(t, [])))) or "-"))
else:
    for t in a:
        t = int(t, 0)
        v = CG.get(str(t)) or CG.get(hex(t)) or {}
        cal = []
        for c in v.get("i", []):
            try:
                cal.append(int(c, 0) if isinstance(c, str) else int(c))
            except Exception:
                pass
        print("%s imp=[%s] callees=[%s]" % (hx(t), ",".join(v.get("m", [])), " ".join(hx(x) for x in cal)))

`tools/_q.py`:

```py
import json, sys, re
cg = json.load(open(r'D:\workspace\ACE\out\cg.json'))
rev = {}
for k, v in cg.items():
    for c in v['i']:
        rev.setdefault(str(c), []).append(int(k))
def s(x): return int(x, 0) if isinstance(x, str) else x
def info(a):
    a = str(s(a)); v = cg.get(a)
    if not v: print('no node', a); return
    print('addr', a)
    print('imports:', v['m'])
    print('callees:', ' '.join(f'{c:08x}' for c in v['i']))
    print('callers:', ' '.join(f'{c:08x}' for c in rev.get(a, [])))
def callers(a):
    a = str(s(a)); print(' '.join(f'{c:08x}' for c in rev.get(a, [])))
def callees(a):
    a = str(s(a)); v = cg.get(a) or {}
    print(' '.join(f'{c:08x}' for c in v.get('i', [])))
def who(a):
    a = s(a)
    for k, v in cg.items():
        if a in v['i']:
            print(f'{int(k):08x}')
if __name__ == '__main__':
    cmd, arg = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    {'i': info, 'c': callers, 'e': callees, 'w': who}[cmd](arg)

`tools/adrp.py`:

```py
import struct, sys
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

PATH = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
e = ELFFile(open(PATH, "rb"))
sec = e.get_section_by_name(".text")
base = sec["sh_addr"]
code = sec.data()
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True

lo = int(sys.argv[1], 16)
hi = int(sys.argv[2], 16)
target = int(sys.argv[3], 16)
window = int(sys.argv[4], 0) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 0x800

insns = list(md.disasm(code, base))
for i, ins in enumerate(insns):
    if ins.mnemonic != "adrp":
        continue
    try:
        tgt = ins.operands[1].imm
    except Exception:
        continue
    if not (lo <= tgt < hi):
        continue
    for j, nxt in enumerate(insns[i + 1:i + 8]):
        if nxt.operands and nxt.operands[0].type == 1:
            pass
        mem = None
        try:
            if nxt.mnemonic in ("ldr", "str", "add", "ldrb", "strb"):
                for op in nxt.operands:
                    if op.type == 3:
                        mem = op
        except Exception:
            pass
        if mem is None:
            continue
        addr = tgt + mem.mem.disp
        if abs(addr - target) <= window:
            print("0x%x: %s %s   (-> 0x%x)" % (ins.address, nxt.mnemonic, nxt.op_str, addr))

`tools/adrp2.py`:

```py
import struct, sys
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

PATH = sys.argv[1]
lo = int(sys.argv[2], 16)
hi = int(sys.argv[3], 16)
target = int(sys.argv[4], 16)
window = int(sys.argv[5], 0) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 0x800

e = ELFFile(open(PATH, "rb"))
sec = e.get_section_by_name(".text")
base = sec["sh_addr"]
code = sec.data()
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True

insns = list(md.disasm(code, base))
for i, ins in enumerate(insns):
    if ins.mnemonic != "adrp":
        continue
    try:
        tgt = ins.operands[1].imm
    except Exception:
        continue
    if not (lo <= tgt < hi):
        continue
    for nxt in insns[i + 1:i + 8]:
        mem = None
        try:
            if nxt.mnemonic in ("ldr", "str", "add", "ldrb", "strb"):
                for op in nxt.operands:
                    if op.type == 3:
                        mem = op
        except Exception:
            pass
        if mem is None:
            continue
        addr = tgt + mem.mem.disp
        if abs(addr - target) <= window:
            print("0x%x: %s %s   (-> 0x%x)" % (ins.address, nxt.mnemonic, nxt.op_str, addr))

`tools/ascii_strs.py`:

```py
import re
import sys

from so_elf import So

path = sys.argv[1]
minlen = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 5
pat = re.compile(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else None

so = So(path)
data = so.data
hits = 0
for va0, end, fo in so.segs:
    i = fo
    limit = fo + (end - va0)
    while i < limit:
        j = i
        while j < limit and 32 <= data[j] < 127:
            j += 1
        if j - i >= minlen:
            s = data[i:j].decode("latin1")
            if not pat or pat.search(s):
                print("%08x %s" % (va0 + (i - fo), s))
                hits += 1
        i = j + 1
print("hits %d" % hits)

```

`tools/bl_callers.py`:

```py
import sys

from so_elf import So

path = sys.argv[1]
targets = set(int(x, 0) for x in sys.argv[2:])

so = So(path)
va0, end, _ = so.sections[".text"]
hit = {t: [] for t in targets}
for ins in so.elems(va0, end):
    if ins.mnemonic != "bl":
        continue
    try:
        dst = int(ins.op_str.replace("#", ""), 0)
    except ValueError:
        continue
    if dst in targets:
        hit[dst].append(ins.address)

for t in sorted(targets):
    print("%08x callers %d" % (t, len(hit[t])))
    for a in hit[t][:50]:
        print("  %08x" % a)

`tools/blob.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ACE (libtersafe.so) 云端下发 blob 静态解析器.

用途: 解析 ano_tmp 目录中的 ob_x/ob_cs2 容器 (a32/a64)、内部 .img 载荷、
comm.dat (tlf 容器)、mrpcs*.data (zip 包) 与 *.sig 签名块.

用法:
  python blob.py info                     # 所有 blob 概览
  python blob.py sections <file>          # 容器段表 (名称/偏移/长度)
  python blob.py img <file> [sec]         # .img 头部与内部表
  python blob.py names <file>             # .img 导入/符号表
  python blob.py strings <file>           # 多相位 XOR 字符串提取
  python blob.py comm                     # comm.dat 结构
  python blob.py mrpcs                    # mrpcs*.data zip 结构
  python blob.py sig                      # 签名块结构
  python blob.py stats <file>             # 熵/字节分布

关键常量来源 (libtersafe.so):
  容器 magic   0x20220118  (= sub_4A0198 读 u32 后比较 539099416)
  段名解密     sub_237DC4: pt[i] = ct[i] ^ "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ"[i%36]
  段载荷解密   主循环 case 24: pt = T[ct ^ 0x23], T = libtersafe+0x9A240
  .img magic   0x20220623 (libtprt sub_1528B0 比较 539100707)
  .img 装载    libtprt sub_1528B0 / sub_13C76C
"""
import collections
import hashlib
import os
import re
import struct
import sys
import zlib

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
UNPACK = os.path.join(ROOT, "dyn", "unpack")
ANO = os.path.join(ROOT, "dyn", "ano_tmp_full")

SBX = bytes.fromhex(
    "abe47fa84624589d7db8f09108138060d697ff1569f8c2d9302916ee3ebb83f9"
    "47cd621d4ef47823648fa7747096a575d3393436177392073bc10efa320f03da"
    "be31672aafef8488e5e7d179e9760cccc425a2017bbf6af12e6dc9277a483a9a"
    "555bfe3c0bae896c547c2c42909b1a8dca5f334c6135aa2ba4bc26df85b54fde"
    "0ae80640509f19edb0f2ec6551bac857044dd7a08a56eaf55c71444577ac9312"
    "00e0c3c5bdad6ffc18b10d1b2895f3c772378b871f6eb9b766d0d2d4c6213f2d"
    "1efdf6dc9e8ce25d86101ca1c0097e4a9405d8b49952a649119843a981ce6322"
    "9cb3f714dde18e6859e6db38b2535eebd5cb4b02825aa32041b62ffb3dcfe36b"
)
SKEY = 0x23
XKEY = b"0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ"
CTR_MAGIC = 0x20220118
IMG_MAGIC = 0x20220623


def sbx_dec(buf):
    return bytes(SBX[c ^ SKEY] for c in buf)


def sbx_enc(buf):
    t = bytes(SBX.index(i) for i in range(256))
    return bytes(t[c ^ SKEY] for c in buf)


def xk(buf, phase=0):
    return bytes(c ^ XKEY[(i + phase) % 36] for i, c in enumerate(buf))


def be32(b, o=0):
    return struct.unpack_from(">I", b, o)[0]


def le32(b, o=0):
    return struct.unpack_from("<I", b, o)[0]


def parse_container(buf):
    """返回 (sections, info). sections = [(name, payload_plain, off, raw)]"""
    if be32(buf, 0) != CTR_MAGIC:
        raise ValueError("bad container magic %08x" % be32(buf, 0))
    n = be32(buf, 4)
    pos = 8
    secs = []
    for _ in range(n):
        ln = be32(buf, pos)
        pos += 4
        name = xk(buf[pos:pos + ln]).decode("latin1")
        pos += ln
        plen = be32(buf, pos)
        pos += 4
        raw = buf[pos:pos + plen]
        secs.append((name, sbx_dec(raw), pos, raw))
        pos += plen
    tail = be32(buf, pos)
    return secs, {"nsec": n, "end": pos, "tail_magic": tail, "size": len(buf)}


def parse_img(p):
    """解析 S 盒解密后的 .img 载荷头. 返回 dict."""
    h = {"magic": le32(p, 0), "f04": le32(p, 4), "f08": le32(p, 8),
         "f0c": le32(p, 12), "f10": le32(p, 16), "countA": le32(p, 0x14),
         "countB": le32(p, 0x18), "countC": le32(p, 0x1C), "countD": le32(p, 0x20),
         "size": len(p)}
    pos = 0x24
    h["pairs"] = [struct.unpack_from("<II", p, pos + 8 * i) for i in range(h["countA"])]
    pos += 8 * h["countA"]
    h["handlers"] = []
    for _ in range(h["countB"]):
        eid = le32(p, pos)
        ln = le32(p, pos + 4)
        raw = p[pos + 8:pos + 8 + ln]
        h["handlers"].append((eid, xk(raw).decode("latin1"), raw))
        pos += 8 + ln
    h["afterB"] = pos
    h["countC_entries"] = []
    for _ in range(h["countC"]):
        ln = le32(p, pos)
        s = xk(p[pos + 4:pos + 4 + ln]).decode("latin1")
        pos += 4 + ln
        a, b = le32(p, pos), le32(p, pos + 4)
        pos += 8
        h["countC_entries"].append((s, a, b))
    h["countD_entries"] = []
    for _ in range(h["countD"]):
        h["countD_entries"].append(struct.unpack_from("<II", p, pos))
        pos += 8
    h["afterD"] = pos
    h["blob_len"] = le32(p, pos) if pos + 4 <= len(p) else None
    return h


def iter_runs(buf, minlen=6):
    cur = bytearray()
    start = 0
    for i, c in enumerate(buf):
        if 32 <= c < 127:
            if not cur:
                start = i
            cur.append(c)
        else:
            if len(cur) >= minlen:
                yield start, bytes(cur)
            cur = bytearray()
    if len(cur) >= minlen:
        yield start, bytes(cur)


PATH_RE = re.compile(rb"(?:/[A-Za-z0-9_.$@+-]{2,}){2,}")
PKG_RE = re.compile(rb"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{2,}(?:\.[a-z0-9_]{2,}){2,}")
SO_RE = re.compile(rb"[A-Za-z0-9_+-]{3,}\.so\b")


def scan_xor_strings(buf, phases=range(36)):
    """多相位 XOR 后按正则捞路径/包名/so. 返回 {string: phase}"""
    out = {}
    for ph in phases:
        x = xk(buf, ph)
        for rx, mn in ((PATH_RE, 8), (PKG_RE, 10), (SO_RE, 7)):
            for m in rx.finditer(x):
                s = m.group().decode("latin1")
                if len(s) >= mn:
                    out.setdefault(s, ph)
    return out


def entropy(b):
    if not b:
        return 0.0
    c = collections.Counter(b)
    import math
    return -sum(v / len(b) * math.log2(v / len(b)) for v in c.values())


def dump_zip(path):
    """手写 zip 中央目录解析, 返回 [(name, method, csize, usize, data_raw, data_dec)]"""
    d = open(path, "rb").read()
    eocd = d.rfind(b"PK\x05\x06")
    if eocd < 0:
        return [], d
    n, cd_off = struct.unpack_from("<HH", d, eocd + 10)[0], struct.unpack_from("<I", d, eocd + 16)[0]
    cnt = struct.unpack_from("<H", d, eocd + 10)[0]
    out = []
    p = cd_off
    for _ in range(cnt):
        if d[p:p + 4] != b"PK\x01\x02":
            break
        (method,) = struct.unpack_from("<H", d, p + 10)
        csize, usize = struct.unpack_from("<II", d, p + 20)
        nlen, elen, clen = struct.unpack_from("<HHH", d, p + 28)
        lho = struct.unpack_from("<I", d, p + 42)[0]
        name = d[p + 46:p + 46 + nlen].decode("latin1")
        if lho + 30 <= len(d):
            ln2, le2 = struct.unpack_from("<HH", d, lho + 26)
            start = lho + 30 + ln2 + le2
            raw = d[start:start + csize]
        else:
            raw = b""
        try:
            dec = zlib.decompress(raw, -15) if method == 8 else raw
        except Exception:
            dec = b""
        out.append((name, method, csize, usize, raw, dec))
        p += 46 + nlen + elen + clen
    return out, d


def cmd_info(argv):
    files = [
        ("ob_x_a64.dat", os.path.join(UNPACK, "ob_x_a64.dat")),
        ("ob_x_a32.dat", os.path.join(UNPACK, "ob_x_a32.dat")),
        ("ob_cs2_a64.dat", os.path.join(UNPACK, "ob_cs2_a64.dat")),
        ("ob_cs2_a32.dat", os.path.join(UNPACK, "ob_cs2_a32.dat")),
    ]
    print("%-16s %8s %5s %10s %10s %6s %6s" % ("file", "size", "nsec", "end", "tailmagic", "ent", "z83"))
    for label, p in files:
        d = open(p, "rb").read()
        secs, info = parse_container(d)
        z83 = d.count(0x83) / len(d)
        print("%-16s %8d %5d %10s %10s %6.3f %6.3f" % (
            label, len(d), info["nsec"], hex(info["end"]), hex(info["tail_magic"]),
            entropy(d), z83))
        for i, (nm, pay, off, raw) in enumerate(secs):
            h = parse_img(pay)
            print("   sec%d %-22s off=%#08x len=%6d img_magic=%08x cA=%3d cB=%3d cC=%d cD=%d code@%#x" % (
                i, nm, off, len(pay), h["magic"], h["countA"], h["countB"], h["countC"], h["countD"],
                h["afterD"]))


def cmd_sections(argv):
    p = argv[0]
    d = open(p, "rb").read()
    secs, info = parse_container(d)
    print("magic=%08x nsec=%d size=%d end=%#x tail=%08x" % (
        be32(d, 0), info["nsec"], len(d), info["end"], info["tail_magic"]))
    for i, (nm, pay, off, raw) in enumerate(secs):
        print("sec%d name=%-22s off=%#08x len=%6d md5=%s" % (
            i, nm, off, len(pay), hashlib.md5(pay).hexdigest()[:16]))


def cmd_img(argv):
    p = argv[0]
    want = argv[1] if len(argv) > 1 else None
    d = open(p, "rb").read()
    secs, info = parse_container(d)
    for nm, pay, off, raw in secs:
        if want and want not in nm:
            continue
        h = parse_img(pay)
        print("== %s len=%d" % (nm, len(pay)))
        print("   +00 magic=%08x  +04=%d +08=%d +0c=%d +10=%d" % (
            h["magic"], h["f04"], h["f08"], h["f0c"], h["f10"]))
        print("   +14 cA=%d +18 cB=%d +1c cC=%d +20 cD=%d  tables_end=%#x blob_len=%s" % (
            h["countA"], h["countB"], h["countC"], h["countD"], h["afterD"], h["blob_len"]))
        print("   pairs:", h["pairs"])
        print("   handlers(%d):" % len(h["handlers"]))
        for eid, s, _ in h["handlers"][:24]:
            print("      id=%-6d %r" % (eid, s))


def cmd_names(argv):
    p = argv[0]
    d = open(p, "rb").read()
    secs, _ = parse_container(d)
    allnames = collections.Counter()
    for nm, pay, off, raw in secs:
        h = parse_img(pay)
        for eid, s, _ in h["handlers"]:
            allnames[s] += 1
    print("distinct import names: %d" % len(allnames))
    for s, c in allnames.most_common(60):
        print("   %-24r x%d" % (s, c))


def cmd_strings(argv):
    p = argv[0]
    d = open(p, "rb").read()
    if p.endswith(".dat"):
        try:
            secs, _ = parse_container(d)
            d2 = b"".join(pay for _, pay, _, _ in secs)
        except Exception:
            d2 = d
    else:
        d2 = d
    res = scan_xor_strings(d2)
    for s in sorted(res):
        if "/" in s or s.count(".") >= 2:
            print("  ph=%02d %s" % (res[s], s))


def cmd_comm(argv):
    p = os.path.join(UNPACK, "comm_comm.dat")
    d = open(p, "rb").read()
    print("size=%d zero_ratio=%.3f entropy=%.3f" % (len(d), d.count(0) / len(d), entropy(d)))
    print("head64:", d[:64].hex())
    print("u32be:", [be32(d, 4 * i) for i in range(10)])
    print("tail32:", d[-32:].hex())
    print("tail xor36:", xk(d[-24:]).hex(), "->", "".join(
        chr(c) if 32 <= c < 127 else "." for c in xk(d[-24:])))
    res = scan_xor_strings(d)
    paths = sorted(s for s in res if s.startswith("/"))
    pkgs = sorted(s for s in res if not s.startswith("/") and not s.endswith(".so"))
    sos = sorted(s for s in res if s.endswith(".so"))
    print("\npaths(%d):" % len(paths))
    for s in paths[:60]:
        print("  ", s)
    print("packages(%d):" % len(pkgs))
    for s in pkgs[:60]:
        print("  ", s)
    print("so(%d):" % len(sos))
    for s in sos[:40]:
        print("  ", s)


def cmd_mrpcs(argv):
    for fn in ("mrpcs.data", "mrpcs_MVM.data", "mrpcs_com.data"):
        p = os.path.join(ANO, fn)
        ents, raw = dump_zip(p)
        print("== %s size=%d entries=%d" % (fn, len(raw), len(ents)))
        for name, method, csize, usize, r, dec in ents:
            print("   entry=%r method=%d csize=%d usize=%d declen=%d" % (name, method, csize, usize, len(dec)))
            if dec:
                print("      head32:", dec[:32].hex(), "ent=%.3f" % entropy(dec))
                for ph in (0,):
                    x = xk(dec, ph)
                    rr = [(o, s) for o, s in iter_runs(x, 8)][:3]
                    for o, s in rr:
                        print("      xor0 run @%#x %r" % (o, s))


def cmd_sig(argv):
    for fn in ("ob_cs2_a64.sig", "ob_x_a64.sig", "comm_sig4", "comm_sig5"):
        p = os.path.join(UNPACK, fn)
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            continue
        d = open(p, "rb").read()
        print("== %s len=%d" % (fn, len(d)))
        print("   head48:", d[:48].hex())
        print("   tail16:", d[-16:].hex(), " zeros=%d" % d.count(0))
        print("   as ints:", [be32(d, i) for i in range(0, min(len(d), 32), 4)])


def _elf_reader(path):
    """返回 (fileobj, [(vaddr, off, filesz)]) ; 失败则按 vaddr==offset 处理."""
    f = open(path, "rb")
    try:
        from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
        segs = [(s["p_vaddr"], s["p_offset"], s["p_filesz"])
                for s in ELFFile(f).iter_segments() if s["p_type"] == "PT_LOAD"]
    except Exception:
        segs = []
    return f, segs


def load_rsa_keys(so_path=None):
    """从 libtersafe.so 取出两把内嵌 RSA-2048 公钥.

    结构 (520 B, sub_489F10 使用): +0 u32 nwords(64) | +4 u32 未知 | +8 n[256] LE
    | +264 R^2 mod n [256] LE (Montgomery 预计算常量, 已校验)
    """
    so_path = so_path or os.path.join(ROOT, "libs", "libtersafe.so")
    f, segs = _elf_reader(so_path)
    out = {}
    for name, va in (("K1", 0x547860), ("K2", 0x547A68)):
        off = va
        for v, o, sz in segs:
            if v <= va < v + sz:
                off = o + va - v
                break
        f.seek(off)
        d = f.read(520)
        n = int.from_bytes(d[8:264], "little")
        out[name] = (n, d[4:8], d[264:520], off)
    f.close()
    return out


def rsa_verify(sig, data, n, e=65537):
    """sig 为 **大端** 字节串. 明文布局 = 00 || MD5(data) || 00*240 (256B)."""
    if len(sig) < 256:
        return None
    m = pow(int.from_bytes(sig[:256], "big"), e, n)
    mb = m.to_bytes(256, "big")
    if mb[0] != 0:
        return None
    return mb[1:17] == hashlib.md5(data).digest()


def cmd_verify(argv):
    keys = load_rsa_keys()
    for k, (n, f4, scr, off) in keys.items():
        r2 = (1 << 4096) % n
        print("%s @file %#x nwords=64 nbits=%d unknown=%s R^2_le_ok=%s" % (
            k, off, n.bit_length(), f4.hex(), scr == r2.to_bytes(256, "big")[::-1]))
    pairs = [(d, d.replace(".dat", ".sig")) for d in (
        "ob_x_a64.dat", "ob_x_a32.dat", "ob_cs2_a64.dat", "ob_cs2_a32.dat")]
    pairs += [("comm_comm.dat", "comm_sig4"), ("comm_comm.dat", "comm_sig5")]
    for dn, sn in pairs:
        dp, sp = os.path.join(UNPACK, dn), os.path.join(UNPACK, sn)
        if not (os.path.exists(dp) and os.path.exists(sp)):
            continue
        data = open(dp, "rb").read()
        sig = open(sp, "rb").read()
        res = {k: rsa_verify(sig, data, v[0]) for k, v in keys.items()}
        print("  %-16s %-14s md5=%s  %s" % (
            dn, sn, hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest(),
            " ".join("%s=%s" % (k, "OK" if r else "no") for k, r in res.items())))


def cmd_stats(argv):
    p = argv[0]
    d = open(p, "rb").read()
    print("size=%d entropy=%.4f" % (len(d), entropy(d)))
    c = collections.Counter(d)
    print("top bytes:", [(hex(k), v) for k, v in c.most_common(8)])
    print("zero=%.3f printable=%.3f" % (
        d.count(0) / len(d), sum(1 for b in d if 32 <= b < 127) / len(d)))


CMDS = {"info": cmd_info, "sections": cmd_sections, "img": cmd_img, "names": cmd_names,
        "strings": cmd_strings, "comm": cmd_comm, "mrpcs": cmd_mrpcs, "sig": cmd_sig,
        "verify": cmd_verify, "stats": cmd_stats}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 2 or sys.argv[1] not in CMDS:
        print(__doc__)
        sys.exit(1)
    CMDS[sys.argv[1]](sys.argv[2:])

`tools/blobhist.py`:

```py
import collections, pickle
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
lo,hi=0x90EC0,0xFBCB0
# collect NUL-terminated blobs (len 2..200) starting at every offset where previous byte is 0
blobs=[]
i=lo
while i<hi:
    j=data.find(b"\x00",i,hi)
    if j<0: break
    L=j-i
    if 2<=L<=200: blobs.append((i,L))
    i=j+1
print("blobs",len(blobs))
hist=collections.Counter()
assign={}
for a,L in blobs:
    b=data[a:a+L]
    ks=[]
    for k in range(256):
        ok=True
        for t,c in enumerate(b):
            p=c^((k+t)&0xFF)
            if p<0x20 or p>0x7e: ok=False; break
        if ok: ks.append(k)
    for k in ks: hist[k]+=1
print(hist.most_common(20))
pickle.dump((blobs,hist), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\blobhist.pkl","wb"))

`tools/cg.py`:

```py
import pickle, bisect, json
from capstone import *
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
text=data[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
starts=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl","rb"))
startset=set(starts)
pltmap=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\pltmap.pkl","rb"))
sites=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\sites.pkl","rb"))
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64,CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=False
calls=[]
for ins in md.disasm(text, 0x1B6C20):
    m=ins.mnemonic
    if m.startswith("bl") or m.startswith("b.") or m=="b":
        op=ins.op_str
        if not op.startswith("#"): continue
        try: t=int(op[1:],0)
        except: continue
        i=bisect.bisect_right(starts,ins.address)-1
        fl=starts[i] if i>=0 else None
        if t in startset: calls.append((ins.address,fl,t,"int",""))
        elif t in pltmap: calls.append((ins.address,fl,t,"imp",pltmap[t]))
pickle.dump(calls, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\calls.pkl","wb"))
from collections import defaultdict
INT=defaultdict(set); IMP=defaultdict(set); STR=defaultdict(set)
for pc,fl,t,kind,name in calls:
    if fl is None: continue
    if kind=="int": INT[fl].add(t)
    else: IMP[fl].add(name)
for b,tgt,off,mov in sites:
    i=bisect.bisect_right(starts,b)-1
    fl=starts[i] if i>=0 else None
    if fl is not None: STR[fl].add(off)
cg={}
for f in starts:
    if f in INT or f in IMP or f in STR:
        cg[str(f)]={"i":sorted(INT.get(f,())),"m":sorted(IMP.get(f,())),"s":sorted(STR.get(f,()))}
json.dump(cg, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\cg.json","w"))
print("funcs in cg", len(cg), "imp pairs", sum(len(v["m"]) for v in cg.values()))

`tools/cmds.py`:

```py
import json
import struct
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

SO = r'D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so'
d = open(SO, 'rb').read()
BASE = 0x1B6C20
code = d[BASE:0x50DFC8]

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True
insns = list(md.disasm(code, BASE))
idx = {ins.address: i for i, ins in enumerate(insns)}

db = json.load(open(r'D:\workspace\ACE\out\db.json', encoding='utf-8'))


def s(i):
    e = db.get(str(i))
    return e['s'] if e else None


# jump table: 88 int32 at 0x90EC0, sdata relative to 0x1BD078
JT = 0x90EC0
tbl = struct.unpack_from('<88i', d, JT)
targets = {}
for k in range(88):
    targets[k + 1] = 0x1BD078 + tbl[k]

lo, hi = 0x1BD084, 0x1BE16C
bounds = sorted(set([t for t in targets.values()] + [hi]))

for cmd in range(1, 89):
    t = targets[cmd]
    if t not in idx:
        print('%2d 0x%x <no code>' % (cmd, t))
        continue
    i = idx[t]
    end = len(insns)
    # stop at next case boundary in address order that is > t
    nxt = min([b for b in bounds if b > t] or [hi])
    strs = []
    last_mov = None
    j = i
    while j < len(insns) and insns[j].address < nxt:
        ins = insns[j]
        if ins.mnemonic == 'mov' and ins.op_str.startswith('w0, #'):
            try:
                last_mov = int(ins.op_str.split('#')[1], 0)
            except Exception:
                last_mov = None
        if ins.mnemonic == 'bl' and last_mov is not None and 0x4E64D0 <= ins.operands[0].imm < 0x4ED3CC + 0x120:
            v = s(last_mov)
            if v:
                strs.append(v)
            last_mov = None
        j += 1
    print('%2d 0x%x %s' % (cmd, t, ' | '.join(strs[:6])))

import pickle, json, bisect
from collections import defaultdict, Counter

    return fs[i] if i>=0 else None

print("funcs count:", len(set(f for _,f,_ in sites)))

c=Counter(len(set(f for _,f,_ in byoff[o])) for o in byoff)
print("offsets:", len(byoff))

`tools/dec1.py`:

```py
tbl = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","rb").read()

def try_dec(idx, eor, add):
    key = tbl[idx]
    L = tbl[idx+1] ^ key
    if L == 0 or idx+3+L > len(tbl): return None
    p = bytearray()
    k = key
    for j in range(L):
        p.append(tbl[idx+2+j] ^ (k & 0xFF))
        k = (((k + j) ^ eor) + add) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    cs = 0xFF
    for b in p: cs ^= b
    if (tbl[idx+2+L] ^ cs ^ key) == 0xFF:
        return bytes(p)
    return None

# test record 0
print("idx0:", try_dec(0,0,0))

# sequential walk, brute force params
idx = 0
recs = []
params = []
fail_at = None
while idx < len(tbl)-3:
    key = tbl[idx]
    L = tbl[idx+1]^key
    if L == 0:
        recs.append(b""); params.append(None); idx += 3; continue
    if idx+3+L > len(tbl):
        fail_at = idx; break
    found = None
    # try all eor, add but early exit by check byte
    chk = tbl[idx+2+L]
    for eor in range(256):
        # optimize: we can't easily invert, brute force add
        for add in range(256):
            k = key; cs = 0xFF
            ok = True
            for j in range(L):
                b = tbl[idx+2+j] ^ (k & 0xFF)
                cs ^= b
                k = (((k+j) ^ eor) + add) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            if (chk ^ cs ^ key) == 0xFF:
                found = (eor, add)
                break
        if found: break
    if not found:
        fail_at = idx; break
    recs.append(try_dec(idx, *found)); params.append(found)
    idx += 3+L

print("records:", len(recs), "stopped at", fail_at, "of", len(tbl))
for i,(r,p) in enumerate(recs[:20]):
    print(i, p, r[:80])
import pickle
pickle.dump((recs,params), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\recs.pkl","wb"))

```

`tools/dec2.py`:

```py
import pickle
tbl = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","rb").read()

def decrypt(idx, eor, add):
    key = tbl[idx]; L = tbl[idx+1]^key
    p = bytearray(); k = key
    for j in range(L):
        p.append(tbl[idx+2+j] ^ (k & 0xFF))
        k = (((k+j) ^ eor) + add) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return bytes(p)

idx = 0; recs=[]; params=[]; offsets=[]
while idx < len(tbl)-3:
    key = tbl[idx]; L = tbl[idx+1]^key
    if L == 0:
        recs.append(b""); params.append(None); offsets.append(idx); idx += 3; continue
    if idx+3+L > len(tbl):
        print("overrun at", idx); break
    chk = tbl[idx+2+L]; found=None
    for eor in range(256):
        for add in range(256):
            k=key; cs=0xFF
            for j in range(L):
                cs ^= tbl[idx+2+j] ^ (k & 0xFF)
                k = (((k+j) ^ eor) + add) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            if (chk ^ cs ^ key) == 0xFF:
                found=(eor,add); break
        if found: break
    if not found:
        print("no params at", idx, "L=",L); break
    recs.append(decrypt(idx,*found)); params.append(found); offsets.append(idx)
    idx += 3+L

print("records:", len(recs), "next idx:", idx)
pickle.dump((recs,params,offsets), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\recs.pkl","wb"))
for i in range(0, 120):
    print(i, params[i], repr(recs[i][:110]))

`tools/dec3.py`:

```py
import pickle
tbl = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","rb").read()
pairs, sites = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\pairs.pkl","rb"))
plist = [p for p,c in pairs.items() if isinstance(p, tuple) and isinstance(p[1], int)]

def is_printable(b):
    return len(b)>0 and all(32 <= x < 127 or x in (9,10,13) for x in b)

def try_all(idx):
    key=tbl[idx]; L=tbl[idx+1]^key
    if L==0 or idx+3+L>len(tbl): return None
    chk=tbl[idx+2+L]; out=[]
    for eor,add in plist:
        k=key; cs=0xFF; p=bytearray()
        for j in range(L):
            b=tbl[idx+2+j]^(k&0xFF); p.append(b); cs^=b
            k=(((k+j)^eor)+add)&0xFFFFFFFF
        if (chk^cs^key)==0xFF and is_printable(p):
            out.append(((eor,add),bytes(p)))
    return out

idx=0; recs=[]; offs=[]
while idx < len(tbl)-3:
    cand = try_all(idx)
    if cand:
        # prefer unique
        recs.append(cand[0][1]); offs.append((idx,cand[0][0],len(cand))); idx += 3+len(cand[0][1])
    else:
        key=tbl[idx]; L=tbl[idx+1]^key
        print("fail at", idx, "L",L, "bytes", tbl[idx:idx+12].hex())
        break

print("records:", len(recs), "next", idx)
print("multi-candidate offsets:", sum(1 for o in offs if o[2]>1))
for i in range(0, 60): print(i, offs[i], repr(recs[i][:80]))
pickle.dump((recs,offs), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\recs2.pkl","wb"))

`tools/dec4.py`:

```py
import pickle
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
tbl = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","rb").read()
segs, dec = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dec_list.pkl","rb"))
params = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dparams.pkl","rb"))
sites = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\sites.pkl","rb"))

def decrypt(idx, E, C):
    if idx+3 > len(tbl): return None
    key=tbl[idx]; L=tbl[idx+1]^key
    if L==0 or idx+3+L>len(tbl): return None
    k=key; p=bytearray(); cs=0xFF
    E = 0 if E is None else E
    for j in range(L):
        b=tbl[idx+2+j]^(k&0xFF); p.append(b); cs^=b
        k=(((k+j)^E)+C)&0xFFFFFFFF
    ok = ((tbl[idx+2+L]^cs^key)==0xFF)
    return bytes(p), ok, L

res={}
bad=[]
for addr,tgt,val,va in sites:
    E,C = params[tgt]
    r = decrypt(val,E,C)
    if r is None: bad.append((addr,tgt,val,"nolen")); continue
    p,ok,L = r
    printable = all(32<=x<127 or x in (9,10,13) for x in p) and len(p)>0
    if not ok or not printable: bad.append((addr,tgt,val,p,ok))
    res[val]=(tgt,E,C,p,ok,printable)
print("decoded offsets:", len(res), "bad:", len(bad))
ck = Counter((params[t][0],params[t][1]) for t in [tgt for _,tgt,_,_ in sites])
for b in bad[:15]: print(b)
import json
out = {}
for off,(tgt,E,C,p,ok,pr) in sorted(res.items()):
    out[off]={"dec":hex(tgt),"E":E,"C":C,"s":p.decode("latin1")}
json.dump(out, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strings.json","w"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)
print("saved strings.json")

import pickle, json

`tools/dec_find.py`:

```py
import pickle, json
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
text=data[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
starts=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl","rb"))
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64,CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
# find decoder-like functions: within first 24 insns, mov wN,#k then ldrb/eor/strb
decs={}
for f in starts:
    off=f-0x1B6C20
    if off<0 or off>=len(text)-64: continue
    ins=list(md.disasm(text[off:off+96], f))
    key=None; has_ldrb=has_strb=has_eor=False
    for i in ins[:20]:
        if i.mnemonic=="mov" and i.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and "w" in i.reg_name(i.operands[0].reg) and len(i.operands)>1 and i.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            v=i.operands[1].imm
            if 0x40<=v<=0xF0: key=v&0xFF
        if i.mnemonic.startswith("ldrb"): has_ldrb=True
        if i.mnemonic.startswith("strb"): has_strb=True
        if i.mnemonic=="eor": has_eor=True
    if key is not None and has_ldrb and has_strb and has_eor and len(ins)<40:
        decs[f]=key
print("decoders",len(decs))
for f,k in list(decs.items())[:40]: print(f"{f:08x} key={k:#04x}")
pickle.dump(decs, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\decoders.pkl","wb"))

`tools/dec_proxy.py`:

```py
import sys

data = open(r'D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so', 'rb').read()

targets = [(0x964C8, 21), (0x983A5, 26), (0x964C8, 22), (0x983A5, 27), (0x964C8, 24), (0x983A5, 28)]

for off, n in targets:
    c = data[off:off + n]
    print(hex(off), n, c.hex())
    hits = []
    for k in range(256):
        p = bytes(b ^ k for b in c)
        if all(32 <= x < 127 or x == 0 for x in p):
            hits.append(('xor%02x' % k, p))
    for j in range(256):
        p = bytes(b ^ ((j + i) & 0xFF) for i, b in enumerate(c))
        if all(32 <= x < 127 or x == 0 for x in p):
            hits.append(('i%02x' % j, p))
    for tag, p in hits[:6]:
        print('   ', tag, p)

`tools/declist.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct, pickle, re
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
e_phoff = struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, 0x20)[0]
e_phentsize = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x36)[0]
e_phnum = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x38)[0]
segs=[]
for i in range(e_phnum):
    off = e_phoff + i*e_phentsize
    p_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, off)[0]
    p_offset, p_vaddr, p_paddr, p_filesz, p_memsz = struct.unpack_from("<QQQQQ", data, off+8)
    if p_type==1: segs.append((p_vaddr,p_offset,p_filesz))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va
    return None
def o2v(o):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if off<=o<off+fs: return va+o-off
    return None

TEXT_S, TEXT_E = 0x1B6C20, 0x50DFC8
ts = v2o(TEXT_S); te = v2o(TEXT_E)
textbytes = data[ts:te]

# 1) find decryptor starts by 12-byte prologue
sig = bytes.fromhex("fd7bbea9f44f01a9fd030091")
starts=[]
i = textbytes.find(sig)
while i != -1:
    starts.append(TEXT_S + i)
    i = textbytes.find(sig, i+1)
print("prologue candidates:", len(starts))

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
# 2) keep those calling 0x4E4A68 and 0x4E4A7C in first 0x120 bytes
dec=[]
for s in starts:
    blob = data[v2o(s):v2o(s)+0x120]
    calls=set()
    for ins in md.disasm(blob, s):
        if ins.mnemonic=="bl" and ins.operands and ins.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            calls.add(ins.operands[0].imm)
    if 0x4E4A68 in calls and 0x4E4A7C in calls:
        dec.append(s)
print("decryptors:", len(dec))
print([hex(x) for x in dec[:5]], "...", [hex(x) for x in dec[-3:]])
pickle.dump((segs,dec), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dec_list.pkl","wb"))

`tools/disp.py`:

```py
import sys
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM
import bisect, pickle, os

PATH = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
OUT = r"D:\workspace\ACE\out"
e = ELFFile(open(PATH, "rb"))
sec = e.get_section_by_name(".text")
base = sec["sh_addr"]
code = sec.data()
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True

fp = os.path.join(OUT, "funcstarts.pkl")
starts = sorted(pickle.load(open(fp, "rb"))) if os.path.exists(fp) else [base]


def owner(a):
    i = bisect.bisect_right(starts, a) - 1
    return starts[i] if i >= 0 else 0


disp = int(sys.argv[1], 0)
mnems = set(sys.argv[2].split(",")) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else {"ldrb", "strb", "ldrsb", "ldr", "str"}
seen = {}
for ins in md.disasm(code, base):
    if ins.mnemonic not in mnems:
        continue
    for op in ins.operands:
        if op.type == 3 and op.mem.disp == disp and op.mem.base != 0:
            fn = owner(ins.address)
            seen.setdefault(fn, []).append((ins.address, ins.mnemonic))
for fn in sorted(seen):
    lst = seen[fn]
    print("0x%08x x%d  %s" % (fn, len(lst), " ".join("%s@0x%x" % (m, a) for a, m in lst[:8])))
print("-- %d funcs, %d sites" % (len(seen), sum(len(v) for v in seen.values())))

`tools/dparams.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct, pickle
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
segs, dec = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dec_list.pkl","rb"))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va
def o2v(o):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if off<=o<off+fs: return va+o-off
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
dec = sorted(dec)
decset = set(dec)

def extract(s):
    blob = data[v2o(s):v2o(s)+0x120]
    insns = list(md.disasm(blob, s))
    movs = {}
    for ins in insns:
        if ins.mnemonic=="mov" and len(ins.operands)==2 and ins.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ins.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            movs[ins.operands[0].reg]=ins.operands[1].imm
    E=None; C=None
    for i,ins in enumerate(insns):
        o=ins.operands
        if ins.mnemonic=="add" and len(o)==3 and o[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and o[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and o[0].reg==o[1].reg and o[2].type==ARM64_OP_REG:
            K=o[0].reg
            e=None; c=None
            for j in range(i+1, min(i+6,len(insns))):
                jn=insns[j]
                if jn.mnemonic=="eor" and len(jn.operands)==3 and jn.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and jn.operands[0].reg==K:
                    if jn.operands[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM: e=jn.operands[2].imm
                    elif jn.operands[2].type==ARM64_OP_REG and jn.operands[2].reg in movs: e=movs[jn.operands[2].reg]
                if jn.mnemonic=="add" and len(jn.operands)==3 and jn.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and jn.operands[0].reg==K and jn.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and jn.operands[1].reg==K and jn.operands[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
                    c=jn.operands[2].imm
                if c is not None: break
            if c is not None:
                E=e; C=c; break
    return E,C

params={}
for s in dec:
    params[s]=extract(s)
cnt=Counter(params.values())
print("distinct params:", len(cnt))
for k,v in sorted(cnt.items(), key=lambda x:(x[0][1] is None, x[0][1])): print(k,v)
print("None:", [hex(s) for s,(e,c) in params.items() if e is None or c is None])
pickle.dump(params, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dparams.pkl","wb"))

`tools/ehf.py`:

```py
import struct, pickle, json, bisect
from collections import defaultdict
data = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
# sections from readelf-like known offsets
EH_HDR = 0x10C234; EH_HDR_SZ = 0x1FCEc
h = data[EH_HDR:EH_HDR+EH_HDR_SZ]
print("hdr:", h[:16].hex())
ver = h[0]; enc_ptr=h[1]; enc_cnt=h[2]; enc_tab=h[3]
print(ver, hex(enc_ptr), hex(enc_cnt), hex(enc_tab))
def read_enc(buf, off, enc):
    fmt = enc & 0x0F
    if fmt==0x00: return struct.unpack_from(">Q", buf, off)[0], off+8
    if fmt==0x02: return struct.unpack_from(">H", buf, off)[0], off+2
    if fmt==0x03: return struct.unpack_from(">I", buf, off)[0], off+4
    if fmt==0x04: return struct.unpack_from(">Q", buf, off)[0], off+8
    if fmt==0x09: return struct.unpack_from("<q", buf, off)[0], off+8
    if fmt==0x0A: return struct.unpack_from("<h", buf, off)[0], off+2
    if fmt==0x0B: return struct.unpack_from("<i", buf, off)[0], off+4
    raise Exception(hex(enc))
off=4
dummy, off = read_enc(h, off, enc_cnt)
cnt, off = read_enc(h, off, enc_cnt)
print("fde count:", cnt)
entries=[]
for i in range(cnt):
    loc, off = read_enc(h, off, enc_tab)
    fde, off = read_enc(h, off, enc_tab)
    if (enc_tab & 0x70)==0x30: loc += EH_HDR
    entries.append(loc)
entries.sort()
print("parsed:", len(entries), "first", hex(entries[0]), "last", hex(entries[-1]))
pickle.dump(entries, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl","wb"))
print("sample:", [hex(x) for x in entries[:5]])

`tools/elf_dump.py`:

import struct, sys

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
assert data[:4] == b"\x7fELF"
is64 = data[4] == 2
e_phoff = struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, 0x20)[0]
e_phentsize = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x36)[0]
e_phnum = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x38)[0]
segs = []
for i in range(e_phnum):
    off = e_phoff + i*e_phentsize
    p_type, p_flags = struct.unpack_from("<II", data, off)
    p_offset, p_vaddr, p_paddr, p_filesz, p_memsz = struct.unpack_from("<QQQQQ", data, off+8)
    if p_type == 1:
        segs.append((p_vaddr, p_offset, p_filesz, p_memsz, p_flags))
        print(f"LOAD vaddr=0x{p_vaddr:x} off=0x{p_offset:x} filesz=0x{p_filesz:x} memsz=0x{p_memsz:x} flags={p_flags}")

def v2o(v):
    for va, off, fs, ms, fl in segs:
        if va <= v < va+fs:
            return off + (v-va)
    raise ValueError(hex(v))

tbl_v = 0xFBCB0
tbl_len = 66948
o = v2o(tbl_v)
print("table file offset", hex(o), "end", hex(o+tbl_len))
tbl = data[o:o+tbl_len]
open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","wb").write(tbl)
print("first 64 bytes:", tbl[:64].hex())
# stats
from collections import Counter
lens = Counter()
for i in range(0, tbl_len-2):
    key = tbl[i]; L = tbl[i+1]^key
    lens[L]+=1
print("len byte distribution (top20):", lens.most_common(20))
print("total offsets:", tbl_len)

`tools/elfx.py`:

```py
import os
import pickle
import struct
import sys

from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM
from capstone.arm64 import ARM64_OP_IMM, ARM64_OP_REG
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile


class Elf:
    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path = path
        self.f = open(path, "rb")
        self.elf = ELFFile(self.f)
        self.segs = []
        self.execs = []
        for s in self.elf.iter_segments():
            if s["p_type"] == "PT_LOAD":
                self.segs.append((s["p_vaddr"], s["p_offset"], s["p_filesz"]))
                if s["p_flags"] & 1:
                    self.execs.append((s["p_vaddr"], s["p_offset"], s["p_filesz"]))
        self.codes = []
        for nm in (".text", ".plt", ".init_array"):
            sec = self.elf.get_section_by_name(nm)
            if sec is not None and sec["sh_size"]:
                self.codes.append((sec["sh_addr"], sec["sh_offset"], sec["sh_size"]))
        if not self.codes:
            self.codes = list(self.execs)
        self.md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
        self.md.detail = True
        self.md.skipdata = True
        self._rel = None
        self._xref = None

    def read(self, va, n):
        for v, o, sz in self.segs:
            if v <= va < v + sz:
                self.f.seek(o + (va - v))
                return self.f.read(min(n, v + sz - va))
        return b""

    def u32(self, va):
        return struct.unpack("<I", self.read(va, 4))[0]

    def u64(self, va):
        return struct.unpack("<Q", self.read(va, 8))[0]

    def s32(self, va):
        return struct.unpack("<i", self.read(va, 4))[0]

    def relocs(self):
        if self._rel is None:
            d = {}
            sec = self.elf.get_section_by_name(".rela.dyn")
            if sec is not None:
                for r in sec.iter_relocations():
                    if r["r_info_type"] == 1027:
                        d[r["r_offset"]] = r["r_addend"]
            self._rel = d
        return self._rel

    def ptr(self, va):
        v = self.u64(va)
        if v == 0:
            return self.relocs().get(va, 0)
        return v

    def dis(self, va, n=1):
        data = self.read(va, n * 4 + 32)
        out = []
        for i in self.md.disasm(data, va):
            out.append(i)
            if len(out) >= n:
                break
        return out

    def dump(self, va, n=1):
        for i in self.dis(va, n):
            print("%08x  %-8s %s" % (i.address, i.mnemonic, i.op_str))

    def scan_xrefs(self):
        if self._xref is not None:
            return self._xref
        cache = self.path + ".xref.pkl"
        if os.path.exists(cache):
            self._xref = pickle.load(open(cache, "rb"))
            return self._xref
        res = {}
        for v, o, sz in self.codes:
            self.f.seek(o)
            code = self.f.read(sz)
            adrp = {}
            for ins in self.md.disasm(code, v):
                m = ins.mnemonic
                ops = ins.operands
                if m == "adrp":
                    adrp[ops[0].reg] = ops[1].imm
                elif m == "add" and len(ops) >= 3 and ops[2].type == ARM64_OP_IMM:
                    base = ops[1].reg
                    if base in adrp:
                        tgt = adrp[base] + ops[2].imm
                        res.setdefault(tgt, []).append((ins.address, "adrpadd"))
                elif m in ("b", "bl") or m.startswith("b."):
                    if ops and ops[0].type == ARM64_OP_IMM:
                        res.setdefault(ops[0].imm, []).append((ins.address, m))
                elif m in ("ldr", "str", "ldur", "stur") and len(ops) >= 2:
                    if ops[1].type == ARM64_OP_REG and ops[1].reg in adrp:
                        res.setdefault(adrp[ops[1].reg], []).append((ins.address, m + "/base"))
                    elif ops[1].type == 3 and ops[1].mem.base in adrp:
                        tgt = adrp[ops[1].mem.base] + ops[1].mem.disp
                        res.setdefault(tgt, []).append((ins.address, m))
        self._xref = res
        pickle.dump(res, open(cache, "wb"))
        return res

    def xrefs(self, target, kinds=None):
        r = self.scan_xrefs().get(target, [])
        if kinds:
            r = [x for x in r if any(k in x[1] for k in kinds)]
        return r


def load(name):
    if os.path.isabs(name) or os.path.exists(name):
        return Elf(name)
    return Elf(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), name))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    cmd = sys.argv[1]
    e = load(sys.argv[2])
    if cmd == "dis":
        e.dump(int(sys.argv[3], 0), int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 1)
    elif cmd == "xref":
        for site, kind in e.xrefs(int(sys.argv[3], 0)):
            print("%08x %s" % (site, kind))
    elif cmd == "str":
        va = int(sys.argv[3], 0)
        raw = e.read(va, 256)
        print(raw.split(b"\0")[0].decode("utf-8", "replace"))
    elif cmd == "tab":
        va = int(sys.argv[3], 0)
        n = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 16
        for k in range(n):
            print("%08x -> %08x" % (va + k * 8, e.ptr(va + k * 8)))

```

`tools/fill.py`:

```py
import pickle, json, sys
sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
tbl = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","rb").read()
res = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\res.pkl","rb"))
N = len(tbl)
ALL = [(7*t-C, C) for C in range(1,8) for t in range(1,15)] + [(0,7)]
def dec(idx,E,C):
    if idx+3>N: return None
    key=tbl[idx]; L=tbl[idx+1]^key
    if L==0 or idx+3+L>N: return None
    k=key; p=bytearray(); cs=0xFF
    for j in range(L):
        b=tbl[idx+2+j]^(k&0xFF); p.append(b); cs^=b
        k=(((k+j)^E)+C)&0xFFFFFFFF
    if (tbl[idx+2+L]^cs^key)!=0xFF: return None
    if not all(32<=x<127 or x in (9,10,13) for x in p): return None
    return bytes(p)

known = {o: res[o][0][3] for o in res}
offs = sorted(res)
bounds = [0] + offs + [N]
found = {}
failures = []
for A,B in zip(bounds, bounds[1:]):
    start = A
    if A in known: 
        found[A] = (None, known[A]); start = A+3+len(known[A])
    if start == B: continue
    if start > B: continue
    # dfs
    memo=set()
    def dfs(idx, depth=0):
        if idx == B: return []
        if idx > B or depth>200: return None
        if idx in memo: return None
        for (E,C) in ALL:
            p = dec(idx,E,C)
            if p is None: continue
            r = dfs(idx+3+len(p), depth+1)
            if r is not None: return [(idx,(E,C),p.decode('latin1'))]+r
        memo.add(idx)
        return None
    r = dfs(start)
    if r is None:
        failures.append((A,B,start))
    else:
        for idx,param,p in r: found[idx]=(param,p)
print("filled records:", len(found), "failures:", len(failures))
for f in failures[:10]: print("FAIL", f, tbl[f[2]:f[2]+12].hex())
json.dump({str(k):v[1] for k,v in sorted(found.items())}, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\allstrings.json","w",encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)
pickle.dump(found, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\found.pkl","wb"))

```

`tools/fill2.py`:

```py
import pickle, json, sys
sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)
tbl = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\strtab.bin","rb").read()
found = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\found.pkl","rb"))
N=len(tbl)
ALL = [(7*t-C, C) for C in range(1,8) for t in range(1,15)] + [(0,7)]
def dec(idx,E,C,ascii_only):
    if idx+3>N: return None
    key=tbl[idx]; L=tbl[idx+1]^key
    if L==0 or idx+3+L>N: return None
    k=key; p=bytearray(); cs=0xFF
    for j in range(L):
        b=tbl[idx+2+j]^(k&0xFF); p.append(b); cs^=b
        k=(((k+j)^E)+C)&0xFFFFFFFF
    if (tbl[idx+2+L]^cs^key)!=0xFF: return None
    if ascii_only:
        if not all(32<=x<127 or x in (9,10,13) for x in p): return None
    else:
        try:
            s=p.decode("utf-8")
        except UnicodeDecodeError: return None
        if any(ord(ch)<32 and ch not in "\t\n\r" for ch in s): return None
    return bytes(p)

fails=[(9096,12281),(13976,14876),(15390,15736),(19412,19713),(19981,20215),(24110,24473),(26165,26541),(28554,29073),(36671,36953),(44787,44962)]
# recompute boundaries from found
offs=sorted(found)
allbounds=[0]+offs+[N]
gaps=[(a,b) for a,b in zip(allbounds,allbounds[1:]) if (a+3+len(found[a][1]) if a in found else a) != b]
print("total gaps:", len(gaps))
newrecs={}
for A,B in gaps:
    start = A+3+len(found[A][1]) if A in found else A
    if start>=B: continue
    memo=set()
    def dfs(idx, depth=0):
        if idx==B: return []
        if idx>B or depth>300 or idx in memo: return None
        for (E,C) in ALL:
            p=dec(idx,E,C,False)
            if p is None: continue
            r=dfs(idx+3+len(p),depth+1)
            if r is not None: return [(idx,(E,C),p)]+r
        memo.add(idx); return None
    r=dfs(start)
    if r is None:
        print("STILL FAIL", A,B,start)
        continue
    for idx,param,p in r:
        newrecs[idx]=(param,p)
print("new records:", len(newrecs))
for k in sorted(newrecs)[:25]:
    print(k, newrecs[k][0], newrecs[k][1][:60])
merged=dict(found)
for k,v in newrecs.items(): merged.setdefault(k,v)
pickle.dump(merged, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\found2.pkl","wb"))
json.dump({str(k):v[1].decode('utf-8','backslashreplace') for k,v in sorted(merged.items())}, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\allstrings.json","w",encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)
print("total:", len(merged))

`tools/fixtables.py`:

```py
import re

p = r'report/main.md'
lines = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read().split('\n')
out = []
n = 0
for l in lines:
    if l.startswith('|'):
        nl = re.sub(r'`([^`]*)`', lambda m: '`' + m.group(1).replace('|', '\\|') + '`', l)
        if nl != l:
            n += 1
        out.append(nl)
    else:
        out.append(l)
open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write('\n'.join(out))
print('fixed rows:', n)

`tools/hook_devinfo.js`:

```js
const QIMEI = 'libqimei.so';
const TERSAFE = 'libtersafe.so';

function log(tag, msg) {
  console.log('[' + tag + '] ' + msg);
}

function readStr(p, max) {
  try {
    return p.readCString(max || 256);
  } catch (e) {
    return '<unreadable>';
  }
}

Java.perform(function () {
  try {
    const U = Java.use('com.tencent.qimei.uin.U');
    const specs = [
      ['z', 'android.content.Context'],
      ['z2', 'android.content.Context'],
      ['o', ''],
      ['u', ''],
      ['p', ''],
      ['m', 'int'],
      ['x', 'java.lang.String']
    ];
    specs.forEach(function (s) {
      const name = s[0];
      const orig = s[1] === '' ? U[name].overload() : U[name].overload(s[1]);
      orig.implementation = function () {
        const r = orig.apply(this, arguments);
        const a = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments).map(function (x) {
          return String(x);
        });
        log('qimei.U.' + name, 'args=' + JSON.stringify(a) + ' ret=' + r);
        return r;
      };
    });
  } catch (e) {
    log('err', 'U hook ' + e);
  }

  try {
    const Qimei = Java.use('com.tencent.qimei.sdk.Qimei');
    const o16 = Qimei.getQimei16.overload();
    o16.implementation = function () {
      const r = o16.call(this);
      log('qimei.16', String(r));
      return r;
    };
    const o36 = Qimei.getQimei36.overload();
    o36.implementation = function () {
      const r = o36.call(this);
      log('qimei.36', String(r));
      return r;
    };
  } catch (e) {
    log('err', 'Qimei hook ' + e);
  }

  try {
    const Holder = Java.use('com.tdatamaster.tdm.device.DeviceInfoHolder');
    const setS = Holder.SetStringDeviceInfo.overload('java.lang.String', 'java.lang.String', 'int');
    setS.implementation = function (k, v, t) {
      log('tdm.set', k + ' = ' + v + ' t=' + t);
      return setS.call(this, k, v, t);
    };
    const getS = Holder.GetStringDeviceInfo.overload('java.lang.String');
    getS.implementation = function (k) {
      const r = getS.call(this, k);
      log('tdm.get', k + ' -> ' + r);
      return r;
    };
  } catch (e) {
    log('err', 'DeviceInfoHolder hook ' + e);
  }

  try {
    const Tss = Java.use('com.tencent.tp.TssSdk');
    const rep = Tss.getReportData.overload();
    rep.implementation = function () {
      const r = rep.call(this);
      log('ace.report', String(r));
      return r;
    };
    const io = Tss.ioctl.overload('java.lang.String');
    io.implementation = function (cmd) {
      const r = io.call(this, cmd);
      log('ace.ioctl', cmd + ' -> ' + r);
      return r;
    };
  } catch (e) {
    log('err', 'TssSdk hook ' + e);
  }
});

function hookNative() {
  const qbase = Module.findBaseAddress(QIMEI);
  if (qbase) {
    Interceptor.attach(qbase.add(0x2b9d4), {
      onEnter: function (args) {
        log('qimei.2b9d4', 'mode=' + args[3].toInt32() + ' mode2=' + args[4].toInt32());
      }
    });
    log('hook', 'libqimei ' + qbase + ' 0x2b9d4');
  }

  const tbase = Module.findBaseAddress(TERSAFE);
  if (tbase) {
    Interceptor.attach(tbase.add(0x2ccd20), {
      onEnter: function (args) {
        log('ace.2ccd20', 'buf=' + args[1] + ' size=' + args[2].toInt32());
      },
      onLeave: function (ret) {
        log('ace.2ccd20.out', ret.isNull() ? 'null' : readStr(ret));
      }
    });
    Interceptor.attach(tbase.add(0x2ccf54), {
      onEnter: function (args) {
        this.buf = args[1];
      },
      onLeave: function () {
        log('ace.2ccf54.out', readStr(this.buf));
      }
    });
    log('hook', 'libtersafe ' + tbase + ' fake token');
  }
}

setTimeout(hookNative, 1500);

`tools/icall.py`:

```py
import pickle, bisect, re, sys, os

OUT = r"D:\workspace\ACE\out"
calls = pickle.load(open(os.path.join(OUT, "calls.pkl"), "rb"))
fp = os.path.join(OUT, "funcstarts.pkl")
starts = sorted(pickle.load(open(fp, "rb"))) if os.path.exists(fp) else sorted({c[1] for c in calls})


def owner(a):
    i = bisect.bisect_right(starts, a) - 1
    return starts[i] if i >= 0 else 0


pat = sys.argv[1]
limit = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 200
rx = re.compile(pat, re.I)
n = 0
seen = {}
for site, frm, to, kind, name in calls:
    if kind == "imp" and rx.search(name or ""):
        fn = owner(site)
        if fn not in seen:
            seen[fn] = []
        seen[fn].append((site, name))
        n += 1
for fn in sorted(seen):
    lst = seen[fn][:6]
    s = " ".join("%s@0x%x" % (nm, sa) for sa, nm in lst)
    print("0x%08x x%d  %s" % (fn, len(seen[fn]), s))
    if len(seen) > limit:
        break
print("-- %d callsites in %d funcs" % (n, len(seen)))

`tools/inline.py`:

```py
import pickle, json, re
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
text=data[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
decs=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\decoders.pkl","rb"))
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64,CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
def N(ins,r):
    n=ins.reg_name(r)
    if n and n[0]=="w" and n[1:].isdigit(): return "x"+n[1:]
    return n
regs={}; out=[]
for ins in md.disasm(text,0x1B6C20):
    m=ins.mnemonic; ops=ins.operands
    if m in ("bl","b","br","blr","ret","cbz","cbnz","tbz","tbnz"):
        if m=="bl" and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            t=ops[0].imm
            if t in decs:
                p=regs.get("x0"); l=regs.get("x1")
                if p is not None and l is not None and 1<=l<=600:
                    out.append((ins.address,t,decs[t],p,l))
        regs.clear(); continue
    if not ops: continue
    if m=="adrp":
        regs[N(ins,ops[0].reg)]=ops[1].imm; continue
    if m=="add" and len(ops)>=3 and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
        r=N(ins,ops[0].reg); b=N(ins,ops[1].reg)
        if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]+ops[2].imm; continue
    if m=="mov" and len(ops)>=2:
        r=N(ins,ops[0].reg)
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM: regs[r]=ops[1].imm; continue
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG:
            b=N(ins,ops[1].reg)
            if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]
            else: regs.pop(r,None)
            continue
    for o in ops:
        if o.type==ARM64_OP_REG and (o.access & CS_AC_WRITE):
            regs.pop(N(ins,o.reg),None)
print("sites",len(out))
def dec(p,l,k):
    b=data[p:p+l]
    if len(b)!=l: return None
    return bytes(c^((k+i)&0xFF) for i,c in enumerate(b))
res=[]
for a,t,k,p,l in out:
    s=dec(p,l,k)
    if s is not None: res.append((a,t,k,p,l,s))
print("decoded",len(res))
ok=0
for a,t,k,p,l,s in res:
    if all(0x20<=x<=0x7e for x in s.rstrip(b"\x00")) and s.endswith(b"\x00"): ok+=1
print("plausible",ok)
pickle.dump(res, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\inline.pkl","wb"))
for a,t,k,p,l,s in res[:25]:
    print(f"{a:08x} k={k:#04x} {p:06x} {l:3d} {s!r}")

`tools/inline2.py`:

```py
import pickle, collections
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
text=data[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64,CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
def N(ins,r):
    n=ins.reg_name(r)
    if n and n[0]=="w" and n[1:].isdigit(): return "x"+n[1:]
    return n
regs={}; cnt=collections.Counter(); samples=collections.defaultdict(list)
for ins in md.disasm(text,0x1B6C20):
    m=ins.mnemonic; ops=ins.operands
    if m in ("bl","b","br","blr","ret","cbz","cbnz","tbz","tbnz"):
        if m=="bl" and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            p=regs.get("x0"); l=regs.get("x1")
            if p is not None and l is not None and 1<=l<=600 and 0x90000<=p<0x110000:
                t=ops[0].imm; cnt[t]+=1
                if len(samples[t])<3: samples[t].append((ins.address,p,l))
        regs.clear(); continue
    if not ops: continue
    if m=="adrp": regs[N(ins,ops[0].reg)]=ops[1].imm; continue
    if m=="add" and len(ops)>=3 and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
        r=N(ins,ops[0].reg); b=N(ins,ops[1].reg)
        if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]+ops[2].imm; continue
    if m=="mov" and len(ops)>=2:
        r=N(ins,ops[0].reg)
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM: regs[r]=ops[1].imm; continue
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG:
            b=N(ins,ops[1].reg)
            if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]
            else: regs.pop(r,None)
            continue
    for o in ops:
        if o.type==ARM64_OP_REG and (o.access & CS_AC_WRITE): regs.pop(N(ins,o.reg),None)
print("distinct targets", len(cnt), "total sites", sum(cnt.values()))
for t,c in cnt.most_common(25): print(f"{t:08x} {c:5d} {samples[t][:2]}")

`tools/inline3.py`:

```py
import pickle, collections, json, re
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
text=data[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64,CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
def N(ins,r):
    n=ins.reg_name(r)
    if n and n[0]=="w" and n[1:].isdigit(): return "x"+n[1:]
    return n
regs={}; sites=[]
for ins in md.disasm(text,0x1B6C20):
    m=ins.mnemonic; ops=ins.operands
    if m in ("bl","b","br","blr","ret","cbz","cbnz","tbz","tbnz"):
        if m=="bl" and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            p=regs.get("x0"); l=regs.get("x1")
            if p is not None and l is not None and 1<=l<=600 and 0x90000<=p<0x110000:
                sites.append((ins.address,ops[0].imm,p,l))
        regs.clear(); continue
    if not ops: continue
    if m=="adrp": regs[N(ins,ops[0].reg)]=ops[1].imm; continue
    if m=="add" and len(ops)>=3 and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
        r=N(ins,ops[0].reg); b=N(ins,ops[1].reg)
        if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]+ops[2].imm; continue
    if m=="mov" and len(ops)>=2:
        r=N(ins,ops[0].reg)
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM: regs[r]=ops[1].imm; continue
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG:
            b=N(ins,ops[1].reg)
            if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]
            else: regs.pop(r,None)
            continue
    for o in ops:
        if o.type==ARM64_OP_REG and (o.access & CS_AC_WRITE): regs.pop(N(ins,o.reg),None)
print("sites",len(sites))
by=collections.defaultdict(list)
for a,t,p,l in sites: by[t].append((a,p,l))
def dec(p,l,k):
    b=data[p:p+l]
    return bytes(c^((k+i)&0xFF) for i,c in enumerate(b)) if len(b)==l else None
def score(s):
    if s is None or len(s)<1 or not s.endswith(b"\x00"): return 0
    body=s[:-1]
    if not body: return 0
    for c in body:
        if c<0x20 or c>0x7e: return 0
    alnum=sum(1 for c in body if chr(c).isalnum() or c in b"/._-:$%();[]<>@*,+=#!? ")
    return alnum/len(body)
keys={}
total=0
res=[]
for t,lst in by.items():
    best=None
    for k in range(256):
        sc=sum(score(dec(p,l,k)) for a,p,l in lst)
        if best is None or sc>best[0]: best=(sc,k)
    sc,k=best
    good=sum(1 for a,p,l in lst if score(dec(p,l,k))>=0.9)
    keys[t]=(k,good,len(lst))
    for a,p,l in lst:
        s=dec(p,l,k)
        if score(s)>=0.9: res.append((a,t,k,p,l,s.decode("latin1"))); total+=1
print("decoded strings",total)
none=[(hex(t),v) for t,v in keys.items() if v[1]==0]
print("targets with 0 decodes",len(none))
pickle.dump((res,keys), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\inline2.pkl","wb"))
for a,t,k,p,l,s in res[:20]: print(f"{a:08x} {t:08x} k={k:#04x} {s!r}")

```

`tools/inline_strings.py`:

```py
import sys
import os

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from so_elf import So
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM


def imm(x):
    x = x.strip()
    if not x.startswith('#'):
        return None
    try:
        return int(x[1:], 0)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def split2(x):
    return [t.strip() for t in x.split(', ', 1)]


def run(so_path, text_lo, text_hi, ro_lo, ro_hi):
    s = So(so_path)
    d = s.data
    md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
    ins = list(md.disasm(d[text_lo:text_hi], text_lo))
    kcache = {}

    def get_key(t):
        if t in kcache:
            return kcache[t]
        k = None
        if text_lo <= t < text_hi:
            body = list(md.disasm(d[t:t + 48], t))[:10]
            for a, b in zip(body, body[1:]):
                if a.mnemonic == 'mov' and b.mnemonic == 'strb':
                    pa = split2(a.op_str)
                    pb = split2(b.op_str)
                    if len(pa) == 2 and len(pb) == 2 and pa[0] == pb[0] and pb[1].startswith('[sp'):
                        v = imm(pa[1])
                        if v is not None and 0 <= v < 256:
                            k = v
                            break
        kcache[t] = k
        return k

    refs = []
    for i, x in enumerate(ins):
        if x.mnemonic != 'adrp':
            continue
        p = x.op_str.split(', ')
        if len(p) != 2:
            continue
        base = imm(p[1])
        if base is None:
            continue
        for j in range(1, 5):
            if i + j >= len(ins):
                break
            y = ins[i + j]
            if y.mnemonic != 'add':
                continue
            q = y.op_str.split(', ')
            if len(q) == 3 and q[0] == p[0] and q[1] == p[0]:
                off = imm(q[2])
                if off is not None and ro_lo <= base + off < ro_hi:
                    refs.append((i + j, base + off))
                break

    out = []
    miss = [0, 0, 0]
    for idx, va in refs:
        blk = None
        dec = None
        for k in range(idx + 1, min(idx + 14, len(ins))):
            if ins[k].mnemonic == 'bl':
                blk = k
                dec = imm(ins[k].op_str)
                break
        if dec is None:
            miss[0] += 1
            continue
        key = get_key(dec)
        if key is None:
            miss[1] += 1
            continue
        ln = None
        for k in range(blk - 1, max(blk - 20, idx), -1):
            y = ins[k]
            if y.mnemonic != 'mov':
                continue
            q = split2(y.op_str)
            if len(q) == 2 and q[0].startswith('w'):
                v = imm(q[1])
                if v is not None and 1 <= v <= 512:
                    ln = v
                    break
        if ln is None:
            miss[2] += 1
            continue
        p = bytes(d[va + i] ^ ((key + i) & 0xFF) for i in range(ln))
        out.append((va, key, ln, dec, p))

    seen = {}
    for va, key, ln, dec, p in out:
        q = p.rstrip(b'\x00')
        if not q or any(c < 0x20 or c > 0x7E for c in q):
            continue
        seen.setdefault(va, (key, ln, q.decode('latin1')))
    rows = sorted(seen.items())
    print('refs=%d decoded=%d uniq=%d miss(no_bl,no_key,no_len)=%s' % (len(refs), len(out), len(rows), miss))
    return rows


if __name__ == '__main__':
    so = sys.argv[1]
    tl, th, rl, rh = (int(x, 0) for x in sys.argv[2:6])
    rows = run(so, tl, th, rl, rh)
    dst = sys.argv[6] if len(sys.argv) > 6 else None
    if dst:
        with open(dst, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for va, (k, ln, t) in rows:
                f.write('%06X\t%02X\t%d\t%s\n' % (va, k, ln, t))
        print('wrote', dst)

```

`tools/java_grep.py`:

```py
import os
import re
import sys

pattern = re.compile(sys.argv[2])
root = sys.argv[1]
limit = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 60

rows = []
for base, _, files in os.walk(root):
    for name in files:
        if not name.endswith(".java"):
            continue
        path = os.path.join(base, name)
        try:
            text = open(path, encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").read()
        except OSError:
            continue
        n = len(pattern.findall(text))
        if n:
            rows.append((n, path))

rows.sort(reverse=True)
for n, path in rows[:limit]:
    print("%4d %s" % (n, path))
print("total %d files" % len(rows))

```

`tools/jcalls.py`:

```py
import pickle
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
text=data[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64,CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
DEC=0x1D69F4
regs={}
last=[]
hits=[]
targets={}
for ins in md.disasm(text,0x1B6C20):
    m=ins.mnemonic; ops=ins.operands
    if m=="bl" and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_IMM and ops[0].imm==DEC:
        ptr=regs.get("x0"); ln=regs.get("x1")
        hits.append((ins.address,ptr,ln,ins.address))
    # track adrp/add/mov
    if m=="adrp":
        regs[ins.reg_name(ops[0].reg)]=ops[1].imm
    elif m=="add" and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
        r=ins.reg_name(ops[0].reg); b=ins.reg_name(ops[1].reg)
        if b in regs: regs[r]=regs[b]+ops[2].imm
    elif m=="mov" and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and len(ops)>1:
        r=ins.reg_name(ops[0].reg)
        if ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM: regs[r]=ops[1].imm
        elif ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ins.reg_name(ops[1].reg) in regs: regs[r]=regs[ins.reg_name(ops[1].reg)]
    if m in ("bl","b","ret") or (m=="br"):
        for k in list(regs): 
            if k not in ("sp","wsp"): regs.pop(k,None)
print("hits",len(hits))
def dec(p,l):
    if p is None or l is None or l<=0 or l>512: return None
    b=data[p:p+l]
    if len(b)!=l: return None
    return bytes(c^((0x91+i)&0xFF) for i,c in enumerate(b))
ok=0
out=[]
for a,p,l,_ in hits:
    s=dec(p,l)
    if s is not None:
        out.append((a,p,l,s))
        ok+=1
print("decoded",ok)
import json
json.dump([[a,p,l,s.decode("latin1")] for a,p,l,s in out], open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\java_calls.json","w"))
for a,p,l,s in out[:25]:
    print(f"{a:08x} {p:06x} {l:3d} {s!r}")

from capstone import *
import struct
p=r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data=open(p,"rb").read()
# vaddr == file offset for this ELF? .text at 0x1b6c20 file off? check program headers is heavy; assume identity (IDA imagebase 0 and segments contiguous from 0)
md=Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_LITTLE_ENDIAN); md.detail=False
tab=struct.unpack_from("<88i", data, 0x90EC0)
base=0x1BD078
for i,off in enumerate(tab, start=1):
    t=base+off
    if t==0x1BD078: continue
    ins=list(md.disasm(data[t:t+40], t))
    txt="; ".join(f"{x.mnemonic} {x.op_str}" for x in ins[:4])
    print(f"cmd {i:3d} (0x{i:02x}) -> {t:08x}  {txt}")

`tools/look.py`:

```py
import sys,re,json
P=r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\allstr.tsv"
rows=[]
for ln in open(P,encoding="utf-8"):
    k,off,func,site,s=ln.rstrip("\n").split("\t",4)
    rows.append((k,int(off),func,int(site,16),s))
def show(regex,limit=200):
    r=re.compile(regex,re.I); n=0
    for k,off,func,site,s in rows:
        if r.search(s):
            n+=1
            if n<=limit: print(f"{k} off={off:6d} fn={func} site={site:08x} {s[:110]}")
    print(f"-- {n} hits")
def forfunc(f,limit=200):
    f=f.lower().lstrip("0x").zfill(8); n=0
    for k,off,func,site,s in rows:
        if func==f:
            n+=1; print(f"{k} off={off:6d} site={site:08x} {s[:110]}")
    print(f"-- {n} strings for {f}")
def calls(f):
    cg=json.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\cg.json",encoding="utf-8"))
    v=cg.get(str(int(f,16)))
    if not v: print("no cg"); return
    print("imports:", ", ".join(v["m"]))
    print("callees:", ", ".join(f"{int(x,0):08x}" for x in v["i"]))
if __name__=="__main__":
    a=sys.argv[1:]
    if not a: sys.exit()
    if a[0]=="-f": forfunc(a[1])
    elif a[0]=="-c": calls(a[1])
    else: show(a[0], int(a[1]) if len(a)>1 else 200)

```

`tools/maskbit.py`:

```py
import sys
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

PATH = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
e = ELFFile(open(PATH, "rb"))
sec = e.get_section_by_name(".text")
base = sec["sh_addr"]
code = sec.data()
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True

for fn in sys.argv[1:]:
    a = int(fn, 0)
    off = a - base
    insns = list(md.disasm(code[off:off + 0x260], a))
    print("== 0x%x" % a)
    for i, ins in enumerate(insns):
        hit = False
        for op in ins.operands:
            if op.type == 3 and op.mem.disp == 0x3BC:
                hit = True
        if hit:
            for n in insns[max(0, i - 2):i + 6]:
                print("   0x%x  %s %s" % (n.address, n.mnemonic, n.op_str))
            print("   ---")

`tools/merge.py`:

```py
import pickle, json, bisect, os
starts=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl","rb"))
db=json.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\db.json",encoding="utf-8"))
res,keys=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\inline2.pkl","rb"))
rows=[]
for k,v in db.items():
    off=int(k)
    f0=int(v["funcs"][0],16) if v["funcs"] else 0
    s0=int(v["sites"][0],16) if v["sites"] else 0
    if not v["funcs"]:
        continue
    for f in v["funcs"]:
        rows.append(("T", off, v["s"], int(f,16), s0))
def fn(a):
    i=bisect.bisect_right(starts,a)-1
    return starts[i] if i>=0 else 0
for a,t,k,p,l,s in res:
    rows.append(("I", p, s.rstrip("\x00"), fn(a), a))
seen=set(); out=[]
for r in rows:
    key=(r[0],r[1],r[2],r[3])
    if key in seen: continue
    seen.add(key); out.append(r)
tmp=r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\allstr.tsv.tmp"
with open(tmp,"w",encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for kind,off,s,func,site in out:
        f.write(f"{kind}\t{off}\t{func:08x}\t{site:08x}\t{s}\n")
os.replace(tmp, r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\allstr.tsv")
print("rows",len(out))
