`tools/ops.py`:

```py
import pickle, struct, json
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_LITTLE_ENDIAN

data = open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so", "rb").read()
dec = set(pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dec_list.pkl", "rb"))[1])
found = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\found.pkl", "rb"))
starts = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl", "rb"))
lo = 0x4E3CAC
hi = starts[starts.index(lo) + 1]

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_LITTLE_ENDIAN)
md.detail = True
ins = list(md.disasm(data[lo:hi], lo))

cur_handler = None
pending_mov = None
rows = []
for i, x in enumerate(ins):
    if x.mnemonic in ("adr", "adrl") and len(x.operands) == 2 and x.reg_name(x.operands[0].reg) == "x8":
        op = x.operands[1]
        if op.type == 2:  # IMM
            tgt = op.imm
            if 0x1B6C20 <= tgt < 0x50DFC8:
                cur_handler = tgt
    elif x.mnemonic == "mov" and len(x.operands) == 2 and x.reg_name(x.operands[0].reg) == "w0":
        op = x.operands[1]
        if op.type == 2:
            pending_mov = op.imm
    elif x.mnemonic == "bl" and x.operands[0].type == 2:
        tgt = x.operands[0].imm
        if tgt in dec and pending_mov is not None:
            v = found.get(pending_mov)
            name = v[1] if isinstance(v, tuple) else v
            rows.append((pending_mov, name, cur_handler, x.address))
            cur_handler = None
            pending_mov = None
    if x.mnemonic in ("str", "stp") and cur_handler is None:
        pass

with open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\ops.tsv", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for o, n, h, site in rows:
        hs = "--------" if h is None else format(h, "08x")
        f.write(f"{o}\t{hs}\t{'' if n is None else n}\n")
print("entries", len(rows))
for off, name, h, site in rows:
    print(f"{off:6d} {h if h is None else format(h,'08x')}  {name}")

```

`tools/pairs.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct, re

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
e_phoff = struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, 0x20)[0]
e_phentsize = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x36)[0]
e_phnum = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x38)[0]
segs=[]
for i in range(e_phnum):
    off = e_phoff + i*e_phentsize
    p_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, off)[0]
    p_offset, p_vaddr, p_paddr, p_filesz, p_memsz = struct.unpack_from("<QQQQQ", data, off+8)
    if p_type==1: segs.append((p_vaddr,p_offset,p_filesz))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail = True
start, end = 0x4E4000, 0x4EE000
blob = data[v2o(start):v2o(start)+(end-start)]
pairs=[]; prev=None
for ins in md.disasm(blob, start):
    ops = ins.operands
    if ins.mnemonic == "eor" and len(ops)==3 and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
        prev=(ins.address, ops[2].imm)
    elif ins.mnemonic=="add" and len(ops)==3 and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM and prev and ins.address-prev[0]<=32:
        pairs.append((prev[1], ops[2].imm)); prev=None
    elif prev and ins.address-prev[0]>32:
        prev=None
print("pairs:", len(pairs))
from collections import Counter
for k,v in Counter(pairs).most_common(60): print(k,v)

```

`tools/pairs2.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct, pickle
from collections import Counter

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
e_phoff = struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, 0x20)[0]
e_phentsize = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x36)[0]
e_phnum = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x38)[0]
segs=[]
for i in range(e_phnum):
    off = e_phoff + i*e_phentsize
    p_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, off)[0]
    p_offset, p_vaddr, p_paddr, p_filesz, p_memsz = struct.unpack_from("<QQQQQ", data, off+8)
    if p_type==1: segs.append((p_vaddr,p_offset,p_filesz))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
start, end = 0x1B6C20, 0x50DFC8
blob = data[v2o(start):v2o(start)+(end-start)]
insns = list(md.disasm(blob, start))
print("insns:", len(insns))
# build index by address
addrs = [i.address for i in insns]
idx_of = {a:k for k,a in enumerate(addrs)}

pairs = Counter()
sites = []
for k, ins in enumerate(insns):
    if ins.mnemonic != "eor": continue
    ops = ins.operands
    if len(ops)!=3: continue
    if not (ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_REG): continue
    rd = ops[0].reg; rn = ops[1].reg; rm = ops[2].reg
    if rd != rn: continue
    # look ahead <=6 insns for ADD rd, rd, #imm
    for j in range(k+1, min(k+7, len(insns))):
        jn = insns[j]
        if jn.mnemonic == "add" and len(jn.operands)==3 and jn.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and jn.operands[0].reg==rd and jn.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and jn.operands[1].reg==rd and jn.operands[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            addimm = jn.operands[2].imm
            # find def of rm before k within 80 insns
            eorimm = None
            for t in range(k-1, max(k-90,-1), -1):
                ti = insns[t]
                if ti.mnemonic=="mov" and len(ti.operands)==2 and ti.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ti.operands[0].reg==rm and ti.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
                    eorimm = ti.operands[1].imm; break
                if ti.mnemonic=="eor" and len(ti.operands)==3 and ti.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ti.operands[0].reg==rm and ti.operands[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
                    eorimm = ("eor",ti.operands[2].imm); break
            if eorimm is not None:
                pairs[(eorimm, addimm)] += 1
                sites.append((ins.address, eorimm, addimm))
            break
print("candidate pairs:", len(pairs))
for p,c in pairs.most_common(80): print(p,c)
pickle.dump((pairs,sites), open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\pairs.pkl","wb"))

```

`tools/pltcheck.py`:

```py
import pickle, bisect, json
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
base=0
text=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]
starts=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl","rb"))
startset=set(starts)
rel=pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\pltrel.pkl","rb"))
# plt map: verify by decoding stubs
pltbase=0x50DFD0
# decode plt to got map
import struct
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
plt2got={}
for i,(got,name) in enumerate(rel):
    a=pltbase+32+16*i
    adrp=struct.unpack_from("<I",data,a)[0]
    ldr=struct.unpack_from("<I",data,a+4)[0]
    # adrp x16, imm ; ldr x17,[x16,#imm]
    immlo=(adrp>>29)&3; immhi=(adrp>>5)&0x7FFFF
    off=((immhi<<2)|immlo)
    if off&(1<<20): off-=1<<21
    page=(a & ~0xFFF)+ (off<<12)
    ldr_off=(ldr>>10)&0xFFF
    plt2got[a]= (name, page+ldr_off*8)
print("plt sample", [(hex(k),v[0],hex(v[1])) for k,v in list(plt2got.items())[:3]])
print("expect", rel[0])
pickle.dump({k:v[0] for k,v in plt2got.items()}, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\pltmap.pkl","wb"))
```

```py
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
def show(addr,n=40):
    b=data[addr:addr+n]
    print(hex(addr), b.hex())
    for k0 in range(256):
        s=bytes(c^((k0+i)&0xFF) for i,c in enumerate(b))
```

`tools/q.py`:

```py
import json,sys,re
db=json.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\db.json",encoding="utf-8"))
recs=[(int(k),v) for k,v in db.items()]
def H(x):
    return f"{int(x,0):08x}"
def q(pat, limit=60):
    r=re.compile(pat,re.I); out=[]
    for off,v in sorted(recs):
        if r.search(v["s"]):
            fs=",".join(H(f) for f in v["funcs"]); st=",".join(H(a) for a in v["sites"])
            out.append(f"{off:6d} [{v['n']:2d}] {v['s'][:60]!r} fn={fs} sites={st}")
    print(f"=== {pat} : {len(out)} hits")
    for l in out[:limit]: print(l)
if __name__=="__main__":
    for p in sys.argv[1:]: q(p)
```

`tools/q3.py`:

```py
import json
```

```py
import struct,bisect
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
tab=struct.unpack_from("<88i",data,0x90EC0); base=0x1BD078
cases={}
for i,off in enumerate(tab,1):
    t=base+off
    if t!=base: cases.setdefault(t,[]).append(i)
starts=sorted(cases)
sites=[]
for ln in open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\allstr.tsv",encoding="utf-8"):
    k,off,func,site,s=ln.rstrip("\n").split("\t",4)
    if func=="001bcf84": sites.append((int(site,16),s))
for site,s in sorted(sites):
    i=bisect.bisect_right(starts,site)-1
    cs=",".join(str(c) for c in cases[starts[i]]) if i>=0 else "-"
    print(f"{site:08x} cmd[{cs:8s}] {s}")
```

`tools/qcg.py`:

```py
import json,sys,re
cg=json.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\cg.json",encoding="utf-8"))
db=json.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\db.json",encoding="utf-8"))
S={int(k):v["s"] for k,v in db.items()}
def A(x): return int(x,0)
def fs(f, maxs=8):
    ss=[S.get(o,"?") for o in cg.get(str(f),{}).get("s",[])]
    return " | ".join(s.replace("\n","\\n")[:48] for s in ss[:maxs])
def cmd_imp(name):
    r=re.compile(name,re.I); n=0
    for f,v in cg.items():
        for m in v["m"]:
            if r.search(m):
                n+=1; print(f"{A(f):08x} via {m:28s} {fs(f)}"); break
    print("total",n)
def cmd_str(pat):
    r=re.compile(pat,re.I); n=0
    for off,v in sorted(((int(k),v) for k,v in db.items())):
        if r.search(v["s"]):
            n+=1; print(f"{off:6d} {v['s'][:70]!r} fn={','.join(f'{A(x):08x}' for x in v['funcs'][:3])}")
    print("total",n)
def cmd_f(f):
    v=cg.get(str(f))
    if not v: print("not in cg"); return
    print(f"== {f:08x}")
    print("imports:", ", ".join(v["m"]))
    ss=[S.get(o,"?") for o in v["s"]]
    print(f"strings({len(ss)}):")
    for s in ss[:40]: print("   ", s.replace("\n","\\n")[:100])
    print("callees:", ", ".join(f"{A(c):08x}" for c in v["i"][:60]))
def cmd_callers(t):
    t=A(t); out=[]
    for f,v in cg.items():
        if t in [A(x) for x in v["i"]]: out.append(A(f))
    print("callers:", ", ".join(f"{x:08x}" for x in sorted(set(out))))
def main():
    c=sys.argv[1]
    if c=="imp": cmd_imp(sys.argv[2])
    elif c=="str": cmd_str(sys.argv[2])
    elif c=="f": cmd_f(A(sys.argv[2]))
    elif c=="callers": cmd_callers(sys.argv[2])
main()
```

`tools/qimei_scan.py`:

```py
import sys
from collections import Counter

from so_elf import So


def load_table(so, table, tsize):
    d = so.data[table:table + tsize]
    out = {}
    pos = 0
    while pos + 3 <= len(d):
        ln = int.from_bytes(d[pos:pos + 2], "little")
        if ln == 0:
            break
        key = d[pos + 2]
        raw = d[pos + 3:pos + 3 + ln]
        plain = bytes(b ^ ((key + i * 0x53) & 0xFF) for i, b in enumerate(raw))
        out[pos] = plain.split(b"\0")[0].decode("latin1")
        pos += 3 + ln
    return out


def main():
    path = sys.argv[1]
    table = int(sys.argv[2], 0)
    tsize = int(sys.argv[3], 0)
    start = int(sys.argv[4], 0)
    end = int(sys.argv[5], 0)
    getter = int(sys.argv[6], 0) if len(sys.argv) > 6 else None
    so = So(path)
    strings = load_table(so, table, tsize)
    adrp = {}
    calls = []
    refs = []
    imm1 = None
    for ins in so.elems(start, end):
        ops = ins.op_str
        m = ins.mnemonic
        if m == "adrp":
            try:
                reg, imm = ops.split(", ")
                adrp[reg.strip()] = int(imm.replace("#", ""), 0)
            except ValueError:
                pass
        elif m == "add":
            parts = [p.strip() for p in ops.split(",")]
            if len(parts) == 3 and parts[1] in adrp:
                try:
                    va = adrp[parts[1]] + int(parts[2].replace("#", ""), 0)
                except ValueError:
                    continue
                if table <= va < table + tsize:
                    off = va - table
                    refs.append((ins.address, parts[0], off, strings.get(off, "<bad>")))
        elif m in ("mov", "movz") and ops.startswith("w1, #"):
            try:
                imm1 = int(ops.split("#")[1], 0)
            except (IndexError, ValueError):
                imm1 = None
        elif m == "bl":
            try:
                dst = int(ops.replace("#", ""), 0)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            calls.append(dst)
            if getter is not None and dst == getter and imm1 is not None:
                refs.append((ins.address, "w1", imm1, strings.get(imm1, "<bad>")))
                imm1 = None
    print("calls %d" % len(calls))
    for tgt, n in Counter(calls).most_common():
        print("  %08x x%d" % (tgt, n))
    print("refs %d" % len(refs))
    seen = set()
    for addr, reg, off, s in refs:
        if (off, s) in seen:
            continue
        seen.add((off, s))
        print("  %08x %-6s %04x %s" % (addr, reg, off, s[:120]))


main()

```

`tools/qimei_string_xref.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""把 libqimei.so 的取串调用点映射到字符串。

取串函数 sub_1EFB8(off)：调用约定 `mov w1, #off; bl 0x1efb8`，函数原地解密并返回 table+off+3。
用法：python qimei_string_xref.py <libqimei.so> <strings.txt> [out.txt]
strings.txt 为 qimei_table_decode.py 的输出（偏移<TAB>明文）。
"""
import sys
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

GETTER = 0x1EFB8


def load_strings(path):
    m = {}
    for line in open(path, encoding="utf-8"):
        a, b = line.rstrip("\n").split("\t", 1)
        m[int(a, 16)] = b
    return m


def main():
    so, spath = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    out = sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else None
    d = open(so, "rb").read()
    strings = load_strings(spath)
    elf = ELFFile(open(so, "rb"))
    sec = elf.get_section_by_name(".text")
    text = d[sec["sh_offset"]:sec["sh_offset"] + sec["sh_size"]]
    md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
    insns = list(md.disasm(text, sec["sh_addr"]))
    rows = []
    for k, ins in enumerate(insns):
        if ins.mnemonic != "bl" or ins.op_str != "#0x%x" % GETTER:
            continue
        off = None
        for j in range(k - 1, max(0, k - 60), -1):
            t = insns[j]
            if t.mnemonic == "mov" and t.op_str.startswith("w1, #0x"):
                off = int(t.op_str.split("#")[1], 16)
                break
            if t.mnemonic in ("bl", "ret"):
                break
        fn = None
        for j in range(k, max(0, k - 400), -1):
            t = insns[j]
            if t.mnemonic == "stp" and "x29, x30" in t.op_str and "[sp, #-0x" in t.op_str:
                fn = t.address
                break
        rows.append("%06x\t%06x\t%s\t%s" % (
            ins.address, fn or 0,
            ("%04x" % off) if off is not None else "-",
            strings.get(off, "<dynamic>") if off is not None else "<dynamic>"))
    print("calls %d, resolved %d" % (len(rows), sum(1 for r in rows if "<dynamic>" not in r)))
    if out:
        open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(rows))
        print("written", out)
    else:
        print("\n".join(rows))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/qimei_table_decode.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""解密 libqimei.so 的内置字符串表。

表格式：[u16 len][u8 key][len 字节密文]，明文 = 密文 ^ ((key + i * 0x53) & 0xff)。
用法：python qimei_table_decode.py <libqimei.so> [table_offset] [table_size] [out.txt]
默认表偏移 0x6cfa4、大小 9469（9469 条 = id 上界 0x24fd）。
"""
import sys


def load(path):
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        return f.read()


def decode_table(d, off=0x6CFA4, size=9469):
    blob = d[off:off + size]
    out, pos = [], 0
    while pos + 3 <= len(blob):
        ln = int.from_bytes(blob[pos:pos + 2], "little")
        if ln == 0:
            break
        key = blob[pos + 2]
        plain = bytes(b ^ ((key + i * 0x53) & 0xFF) for i, b in enumerate(blob[pos + 3:pos + 3 + ln]))
        out.append((pos, plain.split(b"\x00")[0].decode("latin1")))
        pos += 3 + ln
    return out, pos


def main():
    so = sys.argv[1]
    off = int(sys.argv[2], 0) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0x6CFA4
    size = int(sys.argv[3], 0) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 9469
    out = sys.argv[4] if len(sys.argv) > 4 else None
    recs, used = decode_table(load(so), off, size)
    print("records %d, consumed %d of %d" % (len(recs), used, size))
    lines = ["%04x\t%s" % (p, s) for p, s in recs]
    if out:
        open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(lines))
        print("written", out)
    else:
        print("\n".join(lines))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/regs.py`:

```py
import struct, sys
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM

PATH = r'D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so'
d = open(PATH, 'rb').read()
BASE = 0x1B6C20
code = d[0x1B6C20:0x50DFC8]

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
md.detail = True
insns = list(md.disasm(code, BASE))

lo, hi, reg = 0x27FAE4, 0x280440, 0x222284
sites = []
for i, ins in enumerate(insns):
    if ins.address < lo or ins.address >= hi:
        continue
    if ins.mnemonic == 'bl' and ins.operands[0].imm == reg:
        sites.append(i)

print('sites', len(sites))
for n, i in enumerate(sites):
    name_idx = None
    ctor = None
    for j in range(max(0, i - 60), i):
        ins = insns[j]
        if ins.mnemonic == 'mov' and ins.op_str.startswith('w0, #'):
            try:
                name_idx = int(ins.op_str.split('#')[1], 0)
            except Exception:
                pass
        if ins.mnemonic == 'bl':
            t = ins.operands[0].imm
            if t != reg:
                ctor = t
    print('%2d site=0x%x name_idx=%s ctor=%s' % (n + 1, insns[i].address, name_idx, hex(ctor) if ctor else None))
```

```py
import json

    return e['s'] if e else '?'
```

`tools/relocs_show.py`:

```py
import struct
import sys

path = r'D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so'
d = open(path, 'rb').read()

# ELF64 header
e_shoff = struct.unpack_from('<Q', d, 0x28)[0]
e_shentsize = struct.unpack_from('<H', d, 0x3A)[0]
e_shnum = struct.unpack_from('<H', d, 0x3C)[0]
secs = []
for i in range(e_shnum):
    off = e_shoff + i * e_shentsize
    name, typ, flags, addr, offset, size, link, info, align, entsize = struct.unpack_from('<IIQQQQIIQQ', d, off)
    secs.append(dict(name=name, typ=typ, addr=addr, offset=offset, size=size, entsize=entsize, link=link))

shstr = secs[struct.unpack_from('<H', d, 0x3E)[0]]
def secname(i):
    n = secs[i]['name']
    end = d.index(b'\0', shstr['offset'] + n)
    return d[shstr['offset'] + n:end].decode()

relocs = {}
for i, s in enumerate(secs):
    if s['typ'] == 4:  # SHT_RELA
        n = s['size'] // 24
        for j in range(n):
            r_off, r_info, r_add = struct.unpack_from('<QQq', d, s['offset'] + j * 24)
            t = r_info & 0xffffffff
            sym = r_info >> 32
            relocs[r_off] = (t, sym, r_add)

lo, hi = int(sys.argv[1], 0), int(sys.argv[2], 0)
for a in range(lo, hi, 8):
    r = relocs.get(a)
    print(hex(a), (hex(r[2]) if r else '-'), (r[0] if r else ''), (r[1] if r else ''))

```

`tools/rodata_probe.py`:

```py
data=open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so","rb").read()
lo,hi=0x90EC0,0xFBCB0
import collections
# find NUL-terminated blobs starting after a NUL
res=[]
i=lo
cand=0
k0c=collections.Counter()
samples=[]
while i<hi:
    j=data.find(b"\x00",i,hi)
    if j<0: break
    if j-i>=4 and j-i<=120:
        cand+=1
        blob=data[i:j]
        for k0 in range(0,256):
            ok=True
            for t,c in enumerate(blob):
                p=c^((k0+t)&0xFF)
                if p<0x20 or p>0x7e: ok=False; break
            if ok:
                k0c[k0]+=1
                if k0==0x91 and len(samples)<20: samples.append((i,blob))
                break
    i=j+1
print("cands",cand,"k0 dist",k0c.most_common(8))
for a,b in samples: print(hex(a), bytes(c^((0x91+t)&0xFF) for t,c in enumerate(b)))
```

`tools/sites.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct, pickle
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
segs, dec = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dec_list.pkl","rb"))
params = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\dparams.pkl","rb"))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va
def o2v(o):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if off<=o<off+fs: return va+o-off
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
decset = set(dec); decs = sorted(dec)
decstart = {}
for i,s in enumerate(decs):
    decstart[s]=i

TEXT_S, TEXT_E = 0x1B6C20, 0x50DFC8
blob = data[v2o(TEXT_S):v2o(TEXT_E)]
insns = list(md.disasm(blob, TEXT_S))
print("total insns", len(insns))

# also treat as valid targets anything in [0x4E64D0, 0x4ED3CC+0x120)
LO, HI = 0x4E64D0, 0x4ED3CC+0x120
sites=[]
byaddr = {ins.address:i for i,ins in enumerate(insns)}
for k,ins in enumerate(insns):
    if ins.mnemonic!="bl": continue
    t = ins.operands[0]
    if t.type!=ARM64_OP_IMM: continue
    tgt = t.imm
    if not (LO <= tgt < HI): continue
    # walk back
    val=None
    for j in range(k-1, max(k-9,-1), -1):
        p=insns[j]
        if p.mnemonic in ("mov","movz","movn") and len(p.operands)==2 and p.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and p.operands[0].reg==ARM64_REG_W0 and p.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            val=p.operands[1].imm; valaddr=p.address; break
        if p.mnemonic=="movk" and len(p.operands)==2 and p.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and p.operands[0].reg==ARM64_REG_W0:
            continue
        if p.mnemonic in ("bl","b","ret","br"): break
    if val is not None:
        sites.append((ins.address, tgt, val, valaddr))
print("call sites with const:", len(sites))
print("distinct targets:", len(set(t for _,t,_,_ in sites)))
print("distinct offsets:", len(set(v for _,_,v,_ in sites)))
print("max offset:", max(v for _,_,v,_ in sites))
pickle.dump(sites, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\sites.pkl","wb"))

```

`tools/so_elf.py`:

```py
import struct

from capstone import CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_LITTLE_ENDIAN, Cs
from elftools.elf.elffile import ELFFile


class So:
    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path = path
        self.data = open(path, "rb").read()
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            self.elf = ELFFile(f)
            self.segs = []
            for s in self.elf.iter_segments():
                if s["p_type"] == "PT_LOAD":
                    self.segs.append((s["p_vaddr"], s["p_vaddr"] + s["p_filesz"], s["p_offset"]))
            self.sections = {}
            for s in self.elf.iter_sections():
                self.sections[s.name] = (s["sh_addr"], s["sh_addr"] + s["sh_size"], s["sh_offset"])
        self.md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_LITTLE_ENDIAN)
        self.md.detail = True

    def read(self, va, n):
        for va0, end, fo in self.segs:
            if va0 <= va < end:
                return self.data[fo + (va - va0): fo + (va - va0) + n]
        raise ValueError("bad va %x" % va)

    def u64(self, va):
        return struct.unpack("<Q", self.read(va, 8))[0]

    def u32(self, va):
        return struct.unpack("<I", self.read(va, 4))[0]

    def cstr(self, va, maxlen=512):
        d = self.read(va, maxlen)
        z = d.find(b"\0")
        return d[:z if z >= 0 else maxlen].decode("latin1", "replace")

    def dis(self, va, n, count=1):
        return list(self.md.disasm(self.read(va, n), va, count=count))

    def elems(self, start, end):
        return list(self.md.disasm(self.read(start, end - start), start))

    def funcs_from_eh(self):
        sec = self.sections.get(".eh_frame_hdr")
        if not sec:
            return []
        va0, _, off = sec
        d = self.data[off: off + 0x20000]
        if d[:4] != b"\x01\x1b\x03\x3b":
            return []
        cnt = struct.unpack("<I", d[8:12])[0]
        starts = []
        for i in range(cnt):
            base = 12 + 8 * i
            if base + 8 > len(d):
                break
            loc = struct.unpack("<i", d[base:base + 4])[0]
            starts.append(va0 + loc)
        starts.sort()
        return starts

```

`tools/tersafe_strings.py`:

```py
import sys
import collections
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM
from so_elf import So

GOOD = set(b'/_.%:;=-|,() ')
BAD = set(b'\\^`{}~\'"<>$#@!?*&')


def scan_params(s, lo, hi):
    md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
    out = set()
    last = None
    for i in md.disasm(s.data[lo:hi], lo):
        t = i.mnemonic + ' ' + i.op_str
        if t.startswith('mov w15, #'):
            last = int(i.op_str.split('#')[1], 0)
        elif t.startswith('add w1, w1, #') and last is not None:
            out.add((last, int(i.op_str.split('#')[-1], 0)))
            last = None
    return out


def records(d, start, limit):
    off = start
    out = []
    while off + 3 <= len(d) and len(out) < limit:
        key = d[off]
        ln = d[off + 1] ^ key
        if ln > 180 or off + 3 + ln > len(d):
            break
        out.append((off, key, ln, d[off + 2 + ln]))
        off += 3 + ln
    return out


def decode(d, off, ln, key, E, C):
    out = bytearray()
    k = key
    for j in range(ln):
        out.append(d[off + 2 + j] ^ k)
        k = ((k + j) ^ E) + C & 0xFF
    return bytes(out)


def verify(p, key, c):
    cs = 0xFF
    for b in p:
        cs ^= b
    return (c ^ cs ^ key) == 0xFF


def esc(p):
    return ''.join(chr(b) if 32 <= b < 127 and b != 9 else '\\x%02x' % b for b in p)


def score(p):
    if not p:
        return 0
    s = 0
    for b in p:
        if 32 <= b < 127:
            s += 2
        else:
            s -= 20
        if 97 <= b <= 122:
            s += 2
        elif 65 <= b <= 90:
            s += 1
        elif 48 <= b <= 57:
            s += 1
        if b in GOOD:
            s += 2
        if b in BAD:
            s -= 6
    return s


def main():
    so_path = sys.argv[1]
    table_off = int(sys.argv[2], 0)
    table_len = int(sys.argv[3], 0)
    param_lo = int(sys.argv[4], 0)
    param_hi = int(sys.argv[5], 0)
    count = int(sys.argv[6], 0)
    out_path = sys.argv[7]
    s = So(so_path)
    d = s.data[table_off:table_off + table_len]
    pool = scan_params(s, param_lo, param_hi)
    recs = records(d, 0, count)
    fails = [r for r in recs if not any(verify(decode(d, r[0], r[2], r[1], E, C), r[1], r[3]) for E, C in pool)]
    cnt = collections.Counter()
    for off, key, ln, c in fails:
        for E in range(0, 128):
            for C in range(8):
                p = decode(d, off, ln, key, E, C)
                if verify(p, key, c) and all(32 <= b < 127 for b in p):
                    cnt[(E, C)] += 1
    add = {k for k, v in cnt.items() if v >= 8} - pool
    pool = sorted(pool | add)
    resolved = 0
    rows = []
    for off, key, ln, c in recs:
        cands = [(E, C, decode(d, off, ln, key, E, C)) for E, C in pool]
        cands = [x for x in cands if verify(x[2], key, c)]
        if not cands:
            rows.append((off, key, ln, -1, -1, ''))
            continue
        uniq = {x[2] for x in cands}
        if len(uniq) == 1:
            resolved += 1
        best = max(cands, key=lambda x: (score(x[2]), len(x[2])))
        rows.append((off, key, ln, best[0], best[1], esc(best[2])))
    with open(out_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write('offset\tkey\tlen\tE\tC\tstring\n')
        for r in rows:
            f.write('%d\t%02x\t%d\t%d\t%d\t%s\n' % r)
    nohit = sum(1 for r in rows if r[3] < 0)
    nonprint = sum(1 for r in rows if r[5] and not all(32 <= ord(ch) < 127 for ch in r[5]))
    print('params', len(pool), 'added', len(add), 'recs', len(rows), 'unique_plain', resolved, 'nohit', nohit, 'nonprint', nonprint)


main()

```

`tools/tersafe_usage.py`:

```py
import sys
import bisect
import collections
from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM
from so_elf import So


def load_table(path):
    d = {}
    for line in open(path, encoding='utf-8'):
        p = line.rstrip('\n').split('\t')
        if p[0] == 'offset':
            continue
        d[int(p[0])] = p[5]
    return d


def main():
    so_path = sys.argv[1]
    text_lo = int(sys.argv[2], 0)
    text_hi = int(sys.argv[3], 0)
    dec_lo = int(sys.argv[4], 0)
    dec_hi = int(sys.argv[5], 0)
    table_path = sys.argv[6]
    out_path = sys.argv[7]
    s = So(so_path)
    table = load_table(table_path)
    funcs = sorted(s.funcs_from_eh())
    md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM)
    rows = []
    hist = collections.deque(maxlen=50)
    for i in md.disasm(s.data[text_lo:text_hi], text_lo):
        t = i.mnemonic + ' ' + i.op_str
        if i.mnemonic == 'bl' and t.startswith('bl #'):
            tgt = int(i.op_str[1:], 16)
            if dec_lo <= tgt < dec_hi:
                idx = None
                for j in range(len(hist) - 1, -1, -1):
                    h = hist[j]
                    if h.mnemonic == 'mov' and h.op_str.startswith('w0, #'):
                        idx = int(h.op_str.split('#')[1], 0)
                        break
                if idx is not None:
                    k = bisect.bisect_right(funcs, i.address) - 1
                    f = funcs[k] if k >= 0 else 0
                    rows.append((f, i.address, idx, table.get(idx, '')))
        hist.append(i)
    with open(out_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as fo:
        fo.write('func\tcall\tidx\tstring\n')
        for f, a, idx, st in rows:
            fo.write('%x\t%x\t%d\t%s\n' % (f, a, idx, st))
    print('calls', len(rows), 'distinct_idx', len({r[2] for r in rows}), 'funcs', len({r[0] for r in rows}))


main()

```

`tools/wp2.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct
path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
e_phoff = struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, 0x20)[0]
e_phentsize = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x36)[0]
e_phnum = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x38)[0]
segs=[]
for i in range(e_phnum):
    off = e_phoff + i*e_phentsize
    p_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, off)[0]
    p_offset, p_vaddr, p_paddr, p_filesz, p_memsz = struct.unpack_from("<QQQQQ", data, off+8)
    if p_type==1: segs.append((p_vaddr,p_offset,p_filesz))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va
md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
for w in (0x4E64D0, 0x4E65EC, 0x4EB394, 0x4E6708):
    print("=====", hex(w))
    blob = data[v2o(w):v2o(w)+0x11C]
    for ins in md.disasm(blob, w):
        if ins.mnemonic in ("add","eor","sub","movk","mov"): print(" ", hex(ins.address), ins.mnemonic, ins.op_str)
```

`tools/wparams.py`:

```py
from capstone import *
from capstone.arm64 import *
import struct, pickle
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

path = r"D:\workspace\ACE\libs\libtersafe.so"
data = open(path,"rb").read()
e_phoff = struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, 0x20)[0]
e_phentsize = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x36)[0]
e_phnum = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 0x38)[0]
segs=[]
for i in range(e_phnum):
    off = e_phoff + i*e_phentsize
    p_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, off)[0]
    p_offset, p_vaddr, p_paddr, p_filesz, p_memsz = struct.unpack_from("<QQQQQ", data, off+8)
    if p_type==1: segs.append((p_vaddr,p_offset,p_filesz))
def v2o(v):
    for va,off,fs in segs:
        if va<=v<va+fs: return off+v-va

md = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM64, CS_MODE_ARM); md.detail=True
BASE = 0x4E64D0; STRIDE = 0x11C
wrappers = [BASE + STRIDE*n for n in range(101)]

params = {}
for w in wrappers:
    blob = data[v2o(w):v2o(w)+STRIDE]
    insns = list(md.disasm(blob, w))
    E = None; C = None
    # find MOV Wk,#imm definitions
    movs = {}
    for ins in insns:
        if ins.mnemonic=="mov" and len(ins.operands)==2 and ins.operands[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ins.operands[1].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            movs[ins.operands[0].reg] = ins.operands[1].imm
    for i,ins in enumerate(insns):
        ops=ins.operands
        if ins.mnemonic=="add" and len(ops)==3 and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[0].reg==ops[1].reg and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_IMM:
            C = ops[2].imm
        if ins.mnemonic=="eor" and len(ops)==3 and ops[0].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[1].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[2].type==ARM64_OP_REG and ops[0].reg==ops[1].reg:
            if ops[2].reg in movs: E = movs[ops[2].reg]
    params[w] = (E, C, [ins.mnemonic for ins in insns][:6])
print("wrappers:", len(params))
print("no C:", [hex(w) for w,(e,c,p) in params.items() if c is None])
print("distinct:", Counter(params.values().__iter__().__class__ and [(e,c) for w,(e,c,p) in params.items()]))
for w in wrappers[:5]+wrappers[69:72]:
    print(hex(w), params[w][0], params[w][1], params[w][2])
pickle.dump(params, open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\wparams.pkl","wb"))

```

`tools/xref.py`:

```py
import pickle, sys, bisect

calls = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\calls.pkl", "rb"))
starts = pickle.load(open(r"D:\workspace\ACE\out\funcstarts.pkl", "rb"))


def fn(a):
    i = bisect.bisect_right(starts, a) - 1
    return starts[i] if i >= 0 else 0


def callers(addr):
    out = set()
    for site, instr, callee, kind, sym in calls:
        if kind in ("int", "imp") and callee == addr:
            out.add((fn(site), site, sym))
    return sorted(out)


def callees(addr):
    end = 0
    i = bisect.bisect_right(starts, addr)
    if i < len(starts):
        end = starts[i]
    out = set()
    for site, instr, callee, kind, sym in calls:
        if addr <= site < end:
            out.add((site, callee, kind, sym))
    return sorted(out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    a = int(sys.argv[1], 16)
    mode = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "in"
    if mode == "in":
        for f, s, sym in callers(a):
            print(f"caller {f:08x}  site {s:08x}  {sym}")
    else:
        for s, c, kind, sym in callees(a):
            print(f"{s:08x} -> {c:08x} {kind} {sym}")

```
